# Source Research Notes (evidence captured during clone review)

## Batch 1 - Robinhood Chain ecosystem

### SRC-070 chainstacklabs/fomo-solana-rh-listeners (Python/uv) - RH-native onchain listener
- **What it is:** Chainstack reference impl that maps FOMO's onchain footprint (ERC-4337 smart
  accounts with EIP-7702 delegation on RH Chain, plain keypairs on Solana) and ships working
  listeners for the trade flow. Experimental, not production.
- **Key pattern:** RPC-based listeners (`scripts/00_listen_fomo.py`, `01_listen_robinhood_logs.py`,
  `02_listen_robinhood_blocks.py`, `07_trace_trade.py`, `08_wallet.py`). Listens to real onchain
  trade flow rather than an API ranking feed. Docs `docs/02-trade-lifecycle.md` explain the
  two-tx crossing lifecycle (cash on Solana USDC, token stays on issuing chain).
- **For Memeland:** confirms RH Chain can be monitored with plain RPC (`eth_getLogs`/WS) with no
  vendor API - supports reducing GMGN dependence for execution/flow. ERC-4337/EIP-7702 wallet
  resolution knowledge is directly reusable for the whale tracker.
- **Costs:** needs one RPC endpoint per chain (WSS + HTTPS). Reference-only quality.
- **Verdict:** Study the listening/wallet-resolution architecture; Adapt the RPC-listener pattern
  (chain-specific, port to RH/other EVM + Solana). Portability: high for EVM, medium for Solana.

### SRC-076 cvxv666/fomo-robinhood-radar (Python) - fomo profile -> wallet resolution + AI judge
- **What it is:** Full radar app: import fomo.family trader roster, resolve each profile to the
  wallet that *actually* trades, read fills off chain every ~20s, verify every fill's receipt to
  attribute ownership, AI judge for repeatability. Ships Telegram bot + HTTP API + DB.
- **Crown-jewel algorithm (`pipeline/resolve.py`):** fomo's API exposes internal accounts, not the
  real wallet. Resolution infers the executing wallet from co-occurrence: for each of a trader's
  ~12 swaps, ask who else traded that token in the same ~90s window; weight each window by 1/len
  (quiet windows are stronger evidence); the address that appears across most windows is the wallet.
  Accept leader only when clearly ahead (min hits, ratio, 2x second-hits rule). On RH Chain the
  window query is free - filter `eth_getLogs` by the token address; other chains spend Codex budget.
- **Fill ownership (`track.py`):** sources in order rpc (RH, free, keyless, 2 eth_getLogs cover whole
  roster) -> trenches (RH tape) -> codex (multi-chain, costs budget) -> helius (Solana, key). Verifies
  each fill's receipt so a swap delivered by an outside key / dust push is not credited.
- **Honeypot/sellable check (`safety.py`):** two witnesses - (a) simulate a sell transfer with
  `eth_call` to the pair (revert => honeypot), free and works from pool creation; (b) GeckoTerminal
  buys-vs-sells silence (8 buys, 0 sells, pool >30min). Any unsellable token is never a signal.
- **AI judge (`score.py`):** strict-JSON Claude scoring of per-trader context <=2KB: score 0-100,
  status active/watch/dropped, style tags, red-flag tags (bot/bundler/insider-like/wash/one-hit),
  confidence. Emphasizes open-position PnL (realized-only misses memecoin edge). Cheap model, cost
  logging, few-trades => low confidence.
- **For Memeland:** directly attacks whale/smart-money attribution + GMGN independence on its home
  chain. Resolve + fill-ownership + free RPC tracking are strong **Adapt** candidates. AI-judge
  red-flag taxonomy maps cleanly onto the critic/whale voters.
- **Costs:** Python + SQLite + optional Anthropic key; RH path is free/RPC-only. Claude cost is
  bounded and logged.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (resolve/track/safety patterns, port to Memeland TS + shared RPC) - high
  strategic value; **Study** the full Telegram/HTTP app. Portability: RH-first but chain-aware.

### SRC-117 itsnex1s/fomopulse-robinhood-chain-tape (Bun/TS) - public-RPC live tape
- **What it is:** Live tape of top fomo.family trader buys/sells on RH Chain, priced ~1s after
  landing, ~5 min of fills via public RPC, no account. Read-only, no keys, signs nothing.
- **Key pattern:** `apps/server/src/ingest/` - `subscribe.ts` (WS live mode, PublicNode public
  socket default), `parse.ts`, `receipt.ts`, `reconstruct.ts`, `resolve.ts`, `sweep.ts`, `cursor.ts`,
  `lag.ts`; `db/` schema with bags, positions, receipts, prices, trades; `prices/feed.ts` +
  `prices/dexscreener.ts` for pricing. Run via `bun run ingest`, or poll every 12s to avoid WS.
- **For Memeland:** shows a complete, keyless RH-chain fills tape (independent confirmation of
  smart-money flow, zero provider dependency). Fill parsing/receipt-verification and cursor/lag
  handling are directly reusable. Cloudflare Worker + Durable Object is the heavy deploy path;
  single-process Docker version is the relevant one.
- **Costs:** public RPC + optional fomo session token (only for handles/avatars, not numbers).
  Bun runtime (Memeland is npm/Node - port to TS/viem).
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (fill-parse + resolve + cursor/lag into Memeland's whale feed, RH-native);
  **Skip** the full CF Worker/Durable Object deployment for a personal bot. Portability: high to
  other EVM chains.

## Batch 2 - GMGN data adapters and official API

### SRC-100 GMGNAI/gmgn-skills (TypeScript CLI + skills)
- **What it is:** Official `gmgn-cli` (bin) wrapping GMGN OpenAPI, plus a skill library for
  deterministic wallet/dev/token scoring. The skills layer shows how to turn GMGN portfolio data
  into concrete buy/copy decisions without an LLM deciding.
- **Auth (`src/client/OpenApiClient.ts`, `src/client/signer.ts`):** two modes - unsigned
  `X-APIKEY + timestamp + client_id` for market/token/portfolio reads; signed
  `X-Signature` (Ed25519 or RSA-SHA256) for swap/order routes. Signature message =
  `sub_path:sorted_query:body:timestamp`; timestamp validated within +/-5s, client_id UUID rejects
  replays within 7s. **Key insight:** GMGN trade/order routes need your private key - Memeland must
  keep these behind its existing risk/approval gate.
- **Scoring heuristics (skills):** deterministic, chain-agnostic (supports robinhood):
  - Track-record 0-100 from *outcome distribution*, not win rate (a 30% win-rate wallet that cuts
    losses hard can score high - disciplined risk, not luck).
  - Copy-tradeability 0-100: penalizes early entries, thin margins, ultra-short holds, bot-tier
    frequency - "can YOU capture it" (latency/slippage/gas backtest = raw return minus entry
    drift, slippage, gas).
  - Dev-reputation: only when wallet is mostly a launcher (created_token_count > half traded);
    judges survival rate, bonding-curve-stuck tokens, recent security pass; heavily discounts
    track-record/copy scores because the dev controls its own price/holders (self-dealing).
  - Empty-history guard: no force-scored verdict on empty data.
- **For Memeland:** the copy-tradeability backtest + track-record logic maps directly onto the
  whale voter and copy-trade timing. Porting the *algorithm* reduces blind reliance on raw GMGN
  flow and works on all Memeland chains. Official auth scheme is what Memeland must match for any
  GMGN agent-API/trade integration.
- **Costs:** needs a GMGN API key; signed routes need private key. IPv6 must be disabled (IPv4 only).
- **Verdict:** **Adopt** the scoring algorithms (port to TS in the whale/copy-trade voter);
  **Study/Adapt** the signed-order path behind Memeland's approval gate. Portability: high.

### SRC-101 GMGNAI/skillmarket-demos (aitrader reference app)
- **What it is:** `aitrader/app.py` = FastAPI GMGN AI-trader: trending(cheap) -> top-N coarse
  filter -> due-diligence only on top-N -> deterministic hard gates (rug avoidance + consensus,
  run FIRST) -> score/rank (ML placeholder) -> LLM only *explains survivors* -> outputs candidates,
  **never auto-executes**. Separate position escape monitor polls safety/holders and raises a rug
  alert (pure rules, fast). LLM never touches the risk layer or the escape path.
- **For Memeland:** validates Memeland's exact fail-closed philosophy - LLM-as-explainer,
  deterministic gates before scoring, candidate-only output, rules-based escape. Great reference
  for the aitrader integration owner. `memex` is a marketing/memory demo, low value.
- **Costs:** FastAPI + gmgn-cli; default Mock adapter + SHADOW mode so no key needed to develop.
- **Verdict:** **Adopt** as architectural reference for candidate-only + LLM-explainer pipeline;
  **Adapt** the escape-monitor pattern. Portability: high.

### SRC-073 ChipaDevTeam/GmGnAPI (unofficial Python client)
- **What it is:** Unofficial GMGN client that authenticates via **SRP-6a email/password**
  (`srp.py`/`auth.py`) against GMGN's web `/account/login_v3`; step 1 is reCAPTCHA-gated and the
  library cannot solve it (needs an external solver or a real-browser token). Fragile and against
  ToS/ToS likely.
- **For Memeland:** Memeland already uses GMGN; the official OpenAPI key path (SRC-100) is strictly
  better. Password/SRP scraping adds fragility and captcha risk with no edge.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** for Memeland (redundant with official OpenAPI, fragile auth, no edge).

## Batch 3 - smart money / whale / wallet tracking

### SRC-056 ArgosSystems/Smart-Money-Tracker (Python/FastAPI/Discord)
- **What it is:** Full multi-chain whale monitoring platform (ETH/Base/Arbitrum/BSC/Polygon/
  Optimism/Solana): FastAPI + Discord bot + WebSocket + dashboard. 121 files. Heavy, open-core.
- **Valuable patterns (api/services/):**
  - `whale_tracker.py`: clean scanner abstraction - `BaseChainScanner` protocol with
    `is_healthy()/get_latest_block()/scan_block(n)`; `EvmChainScanner` (web3 AsyncWeb3,
    `eth_getLogs` ERC-20 Transfer topic `0xddf252ad...`); `MultiChainTracker` runs one asyncio
    task per chain; `SolanaScanner` overrides with `getSignaturesForAddress` per wallet (far more
    efficient than slot scanning). Price cache with TTL.
  - `accumulation_detector.py`: repeated-accumulation pattern - >=3 BUY alerts same token in 24h,
    >= $50k volume, 6h cooldown before re-alert. Two-phase commit after each whale alert.
  - `twitter/scoring.py`: alert priority scoring matrix (Whale exchange transfer >$500k=90,
    VC=80, smart money >$100k=70, price ATH=75...). Configurable weights, priority queue.
- **For Memeland:** the scanner abstraction + accumulation pattern map directly onto the whale
  voter and whale tracker. But it's Python/FastAPI/SQLAlchemy/Discord - Memeland is Node/TS.
- **Costs:** needs RPC per chain; heavy platform to operate.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** the whale-scanner abstraction + accumulation detector + priority-scoring
  matrix (port to TS/viem in the whale feed); **Study** the full platform; **Skip** wholesale.
  Portability: high (EVM scanner logic is chain-generic).

### SRC-118 jamsturg/crypto-whale-tracker (Python, thin)
- **What it is:** Bare starter scaffold - single `blockchain.py` with a `BlockchainConnector` ABC
  and a basic `EthereumConnector` (get_latest_block/get_transaction/get_balance). README advertises
  features that are not implemented. 2 files total.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** (incomplete stub, no edge over Memeland's existing whale tracker).

### SRC-038 0xuezhang985/wallet-convergence-alert (Chrome extension)
- **What it is:** Non-official browser extension that scrapes the gmgn.ai (and xxyy.io) wallet-
  tracking UI to detect **convergence**: `minWallets` (default 2) known wallets buying the SAME
  token within `timeWindowMin` (default 30 min), tiered alerts, contract-dedup across chains.
  UI-scraping based (`content.js` reads the tracking panel DOM).
- **For Memeland:** the *convergence signal* (N distinct known-good wallets buying the same token
  in a short window) is a useful independent confirmation for opportunity discovery - Memeland
  already receives wallet flows and can compute it in backend from its own data (no UI scraping).
  Also supports bot/coordinated-cluster detection.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** the convergence-alert concept as a shared signal/voter in Memeland;
  **Skip** the extension itself (fragile DOM scraping, not a backend component). Portability: high.

## Batch 4 - rug / honeypot detection

### SRC-080 degenfrends/solana-rugchecker (TypeScript npm lib)
- **What it is:** `SPLRugchecker` class checking a Solana token for rug risk: metadata (mintable/
  freezable/mutable), top-holder concentration, liquidity lock/burn, DexScreener market data.
  `check()` runs 4 checkers in parallel; `rugScore()` = additive heuristic; `isRug` if score >= 80.
- **Concrete scoring heuristic (`src/index.ts`):** mintable +80, freezable +65, mutable +50;
  top-holders% >=50 -> +80, >=35 -> +60, >=20 -> +50; LP not locked +80; per top-holder %
  (>=10->80, >=7->70, >=5->50, >=3->40, >=2->30, >=1->20). Not normalized (can exceed 100).
- **For Memeland:** the holder-concentration + LP-lock + mint/freeze/mutability heuristic is a
  solid cross-chain rug concept that enriches Memeland's existing `rug-scoring.ts`. Solana-specific
  plumbing (SPL getTokenSupply/getTokenLargestAccounts, Raydium pool file, helius) applies only to
  the Solana feed. Note: repo is "under development, may give false results".
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** the rug-score heuristics into Memeland's rug/security voter (esp. Solana
  feed); portability of concept to EVM is high. **Skip** wholesale (Solana-only lib, unnormalized).

### SRC-128 kukapay/honeypot-detector-mcp (Python MCP)
- **What it is:** Thin MCP server wrapping the **honeypot.is** API for Ethereum/BSC/Base. Returns
  honeypot status, risk level, buy/sell/transfer taxes, contract open-source status (Markdown).
- **For Memeland:** adds an MCP layer + external API dependency; honeypot.is does NOT cover
  Robinhood Chain. Memeland already has GoPlus + its own safety voter + sellable simulation.
- **Verdict:** **Study** honeypot.is as an additional EVM confirmation source (cheap, keyless);
  **Skip** the MCP-server wrapper itself (extra orchestration layer, no RH coverage).

### SRC-131 kukapay/rug-check-mcp (Python MCP)
- **What it is:** Thin MCP server wrapping the **Solsniffer** API (needs Solsniffer API key) for
  Solana meme tokens - name, symbol, Snif score, market cap, price, supply, risks, audit status.
- **For Memeland:** Solana-only, external key dependency, redundant with existing rug/bot features
  and GoPlus. Just a wrapper; no new logic.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** (redundant, Solana-only, adds an MCP layer + key dependency, no edge).

## Batch 5 - agent orchestration frameworks

### SRC-207 TauricResearch/TradingAgents (Python, LangGraph LLM pipeline)
- **What it is:** Research-grade multi-agent LLM trading framework (arXiv 2412.20138). Agents:
  analysts (fundamentals/market/news/sentiment/social_media) -> research managers -> bull/bear
  researchers who *debate* -> portfolio manager -> aggressive/conservative/neutral risk debators ->
  trader. Stock/equities oriented.
- **Key pattern:** bull-vs-bear debate then risk debators to challenge a thesis; each agent is an
  LLM node in a LangGraph state machine; `agent_states.py`/`agent_utils.py` manage shared state.
- **For Memeland:** the "debate to validate a thesis" pattern is conceptually similar to Memeland's
  critic voter but far heavier - every node is an LLM call (high cost, high latency, stock-focused).
  Memeland's leaner 7-voter consensus is more appropriate for low-latency memecoin discovery.
- **Verdict:** **Study** the debate/validation pattern and multi-agent state design; **Skip**
  wholesale (too heavy/LLM-costly for a personal low-latency bot, equities-oriented).

### SRC-208 The-Swarm-Corporation/AutoHedge (Python swarm hedge fund)
- **What it is:** Enterprise "autonomous agent hedge fund": Director (thesis) -> Quant ->
  Risk Management (sizing) -> Execution agents. Solana execution via Jupiter (`jupiter_price.py`/
  `jupiter_search.py`), plus Exa search, Polygon, Yahoo tools. 51 files.
- **For Memeland:** the Director/Quant/Risk/Execution division + risk-first sizing is sound and
  maps to Memeland's domain/risk/execution separation, but the implementation is a heavy multi-LLM
  swarm with full autonomous trading (Memeland intentionally keeps execution behind approval gates).
- **Verdict:** **Study** the agent-division and risk-first sizing; **Skip** the autonomous-execution
  framework itself. Portability: Solana-first, no Robinhood support.

### SRC-188 redactedmeme/swarm (1063 files, Solana AI swarm)
- **What it is:** Massive experimental swarm tied to the $REDACTED token: elizaOS-compatible
  `.character.json` agents, multi-provider LLM router, Mem0/Qdrant memory, Telegram/web UI, Sevenfold
  Committee governance, x402 micropayments, "autonomous self-replication", Claude skills layer.
  Heavily marketing/PR driven around its own tokenomics.
- **For Memeland:** no practical edge for a lean personal bot; extreme complexity, token economics
  focus, Solana-specific, and dominated by agent-governance theater rather than trading edge.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** wholesale (too heavy, token-PR-driven, no clear trading edge).

## Batch 6 - execution / copy-trade bots

### SRC-205 Stormeye85/robinhood-token-sniper (TypeScript/Hardhat, RH-chain)
- **What it is:** Despite the name, a **RH-chain token DEPLOYER** toolkit: OpenZeppelin ERC-20,
  Hardhat deploy scripts for mainnet/testnet, Blockscout verify, viem read helpers, wallet add-chain
  params. Chain ID 4663 confirmed; RPC `https://rpc.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com`; testnet 46630;
  explorer robinhoodchain.blockscout.com; gas paid in ETH.
- **For Memeland:** confirms canonical RH-chain RPC/explorer/config and shows viem-based read
  helpers that match Memeland's stack. Not a trading edge itself.
- **Verdict:** **Study** (infrastructure reference for RH-chain network config); **Skip** the
  token-deployer feature. Portability: n/a (already Memeland's chain).

### SRC-156 mortdeus/solana-copy-sniper-mev-trading-bot (Node + Rust, Solana)
- **What it is:** Ultra-fast Solana sniper/copy/MEV bot using Jito Shred stream, gRPC, Helius
  websocket; Rust + Node variants with program-logic diagrams. README is a "star & hire me" pitch;
  warns the strategy is not stable and can burn a deposit.
- **For Memeland:** execution-speed techniques (Jito, gRPC, WS vs shred) apply only to Solana's
  special MEV environment; no Robinhood support. High risk to copy (unstable, marketing repo).
- **Verdict:** **Study** the Solana MEV/WS execution techniques for the Solana feed; **Skip**
  wholesale (Solana-only, unstable, unrelated to RH chain, safety risk).

### SRC-108 harutocodes/pumpfun-copytrade (PumpCopy, .NET/WPF, Solana)
- **What it is:** Well-structured copy-trade bot for pump.fun. Watches whale wallets via WS
  `logsSubscribe`, decodes pump.fun trade events from tx logs (copies actual fills, not instruction
  limits), mirrors bonding-curve buys/sells from your wallet.
- **Strong architecture (src/):** `CopyEngine`, `SizeCalculator` (fixed or leader-proportional with
  per-leader multiplier), `WalletTrackerService`, `WsFeed`, `RpcPool` (failover + round-robin),
  `RpcProbe`, `PumpExecutor`, **`PaperBroker` (paper/dry-run executor)**, `PositionStore`,
  `CurveService`. Filters: min leader SOL, max our SOL, max open positions, skip migrated curves,
  random delay before copying. WARNING: not a stable strategy; can burn deposit; never use main
  wallet. Includes copy-sizing tests.
- **For Memeland:** the copy-trade mechanics map onto Memeland's Solana/pump.fun copy-trade side:
  proportional sizing with per-leader multiplier, WS trade-event decoding, RPC pool failover, and
  the paper-broker dry-run pattern all align with Memeland's fail-closed design.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** the copy-trade sizing/execution + paper-broker patterns for the Solana
  feed (port to TS/Node); **Skip** wholesale (.NET/WPF desktop app, Solana-only).

## Batch 7 - full trading platforms

### SRC-098 freqtrade/freqtrade (Python, 782 files)
- **What it is:** Mature open-source crypto trading bot (CEX-oriented): backtesting, hyperopt
  (ML/optimization of strategy params), FreqAI (ML), risk management (stoploss/trailing), Telegram/
  webUI, dry-run. Structure: `exchange/` (exchange adapters), `strategy/`, `optimize/` (backtest +
  hyperopt), `freqai/`, `persistence/`, `leverage/`, `rpc/`.
- **For Memeland:** the reusable *patterns* are backtesting + hyperopt (systematic strategy
  parameter optimization) and the IStrategy/risk interfaces. But freqtrade is CEX/DEX-agnostic with
  a Python plugin model and is heavy; Memeland is a TS memecoin bot with a custom swarm.
- **Verdict:** **Study** backtesting + hyperopt + risk-management patterns (and FreqAI) as design
  references; **Skip** wholesale (Python/CEX model, too heavy for a personal memecoin bot).

### SRC-113 hummingbot/hummingbot (Python, 1860 files)
- **What it is:** Open-source HFT framework for market-making/arb on CEX + DEX (Apache 2.0), $34B
  traded volume. `hummingbot/strategy/` has pure_market_making, cross_exchange_market_making,
  amm_arb, avellaneda_market_making, hedge, liquidity_mining; connectors per venue; v2 "controllers".
- **For Memeland:** the connector-abstraction and strategy-controller patterns are the valuable
  reference (clean venue adapters + composable strategies). But it is HFT market-making oriented,
  huge, and Python.
- **Verdict:** **Study** connector/controller abstraction; **Skip** wholesale (too heavy, HFT
  market-making focus, no memecoin-discovery or Robinhood-chain edge out of the box).

### SRC-157 muratmula/ai-robinhood-chain (TypeScript)
- **What it is:** "OPENCATZ AI - Robinhood Chain Edition" - this is a sibling/prior build of the
  SAME architecture Memeland uses (adapters/agents/api/cli/discord/orchestrator/position/services/
  telegram + domain dirs meme/nft/whale-eth/alpha-robinhood). Effectively Memeland's own design.
- **For Memeland:** useful only as a comparison baseline for the current repo; no new edge.
- **Verdict:** **Study** (comparison baseline only); **Skip** (redundant with Memeland itself).

## Batch 8 - discovery lists and wallet-data bootstrap

### SRC-170 nirholas/scrape-smart-wallets (browser-console JavaScript)
- **What it is:** Five browser-console scripts that intercept and call GMGN web APIs to collect
  smart-money/KOL wallets, wallet holdings, radar signals, token rankings, token activity, and DEX
  trades. The master orchestrator runs single-chain or multi-chain jobs and exports JSON/CSV plus
  overlap/dedup analytics.
- **Useful implementation patterns:** endpoint interception for discovery, explicit chain/mode
  orchestration, exponential backoff with jitter for 429/5xx, human delays, unified result envelope
  with step timings/errors/missing-script diagnostics, cross-source wallet overlap/dedup analysis.
- **Limitations:** browser session cookies, undocumented GMGN web endpoints, DOM/session coupling,
  and no durable provider boundary. It increases GMGN dependence and can break without notice.
- **Verdict:** **Study** the overlap/dedup analytics and retry/result-envelope patterns;
  **Skip** the scraper as a production service. Use only as a one-off seed/bootstrap tool if the
  shared GMGN adapter is unavailable. Portability: low as implementation, high as data-quality idea.

### SRC-062 BlockRunAI/awesome-finance-mcp (curated MCP directory)
- **What it is:** A curated index of finance/trading/crypto MCP servers and agent skills across
  market data, execution, on-chain analytics, and research. It contains pointers, not an engine.
- **For Memeland:** useful as a discovery map for independent providers and MCP candidates, but
  entries require individual validation for chain coverage, freshness, credentials, rate limits,
  safety, and maintenance. Adding an MCP layer does not itself create edge.
- **Verdict:** **Study** as a source-discovery index; **Skip** treating the list as an integration
  dependency. Any adopted item must pass Memeland's provider and safety gates separately.

### SRC-136 LLMQuant/awesome-trading-agents (curated agent/MCP/skills directory)
- **What it is:** Curated list organized into Agents, MCPs, and Skills, with selection criteria of
  public artifacts, clear LLM behavior, recent activity, distinct role, and visible adoption. It
  highlights TradingAgents, AI hedge funds, Alpaca/financial-datasets MCPs, and finance skills.
- **For Memeland:** good for finding candidates and comparing architecture patterns; it explicitly
  excludes classic quant/RL libraries, so it is not a complete trading-systems inventory. It is not
  evidence that any listed project has live edge or RH-chain support.
- **Verdict:** **Study** as a research index; **Skip** direct integration based on listing alone.

## Batch 9 - Robinhood chain: MCP servers, CLI sniper, LP bot, trading toolkit

### SRC-169 nirholas/robinhood-chain-mcp (TypeScript MCP servers)
- **What it is:** A pair of MCP servers for the Robinhood chain. The **data server** is keyless
  public-RPC reads (chain stats, stock tokens, trending, launches via on-chain logs), pulling
  secondary data from DeFiLlama, Blockscout (`robinhoodchain.blockscout.com`) and GeckoTerminal
  (`network=robinhood`). The **trading server** is opt-in only: enabled via a kill-switch env
  (`HOOD_MCP_ENABLE_TRADING=1` + private key), enforces a Stock-Token eligibility gate, caps USD
  spend via a `SpendLedger.assertWithinCaps` check *before* signing (per-call and per-session), and
  uses a confirm gate (preview -> `confirm:true` -> broadcast). License: all-rights-reserved (noted
  only; ignored for personal use).
- **For Memeland:** the execution-hardening *guard model* is the reusable asset: env kill-switch,
  eligibility gate, pre-sign spend caps, and a human/agent confirm gate before broadcast.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** the execution-guard model (kill-switch env gating, spend caps, confirm
  gate) into Memeland's execution layer; **Study** the keyless data-server reads as a reference for
  Memeland's own RH RPC readers. Not a wholesale adoption.

### SRC-180 pamgarcia1993/robinhood-lp-bot (TypeScript, ethers v6 + Uniswap SDK, MIT) - best RH source so far
- **What it is:** A Robinhood-chain LP bot. The reusable pieces live in `src/`:
  - `chain/sequencer.ts` - **fast-submit** directly to `https://sequencer.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com/`
    (accepts only `eth_sendRawTransaction`; ~8ms hop saved; IP-pins the host to avoid DNS hijack).
  - `feed/listener.ts` + `feed/decode.ts` - **Arbitrum Nitro broadcast feed**
    `wss://feed.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com/feed` with header `Arbitrum-Feed-Client-Version: 2`,
    giving sub-second pre-RPC tape; includes an IP-pin workaround for the same host.
  - `watch/scanner.ts` - **Quoter honeypot**: buy 0.01 ETH then sell back via `quoteExactInputSingle`
    (the only real solver on chain 4663), plus a volume-spike detector (rising vs previous, not just
    high) from the Blockscout token list + batched-30 DexScreener calls, with a stablecoin filter.
  - `chain/txlock.ts` - global wallet-tx serializer to prevent nonce-collision during multi-tx
    open/close.
  - `radar/gmgn.ts` - graceful `gmgn-cli` enrichment (chain `robinhood`) that degrades to `null`
    when the CLI is absent.
  - 350ms receipt polling; owner-only auth; atomic file writes; auto-farming gates.
- **For Memeland:** the most directly reusable RH source found. Fast-submit sequencer, Nitro
  broadcast-feed tape listener, Quoter honeypot (honeypot detection that works even where block
  explorers lag), txlock nonce serializer, and graceful GMGN enrichment all map onto Memeland's RH
  execution and scanning layers.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** fast-submit sequencer, Nitro feed listener, Quoter honeypot, and txlock
  serializer; **Adopt** it as the reference architecture for Memeland's Robinhood-chain execution
  layer.

### SRC-199 sirenconemd/robinhood-trading-toolkit (closed-source Windows desktop app)
- **What it is:** "RobinBundler" - a closed Windows desktop app. README-only (no code shipped);
  bundler/volume-tool marketing. Proprietary; nothing adoptable or auditable.
- **For Memeland:** nothing reusable; no source to deep-read.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** (proprietary, no code). Documented as a dead end after checking for code.

### SRC-212 Thorsten02041973/robinhood-cli ("Hitman", Node CLI sniper)
- **What it is:** A Node.js CLI sniper on RH mainnet using web3.js. Confirms key RH constants:
  chain ID **4663**, RPC `https://rpc.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com`, testnet 46630, WETH
  `0x0Bd7D308f8E1639FAb988df18A8011f41EAcAD73`, USDG `0x5fc5360D0400a0Fd4f2af552ADD042D716F1d168`
  (6 decimals), **UniswapV2Router02 `0x89e5DB8B5aA49aA85AC63f691524311AEB649eba`**. Has dry-run,
  `router-guard.ts` (asserts a router is configured), RPC health-check + fallback, and preflight
  validation. Weaknesses: private key stored plaintext in `~/Documents/defi-cli-configs`, web3.js,
  basic architecture.
- **For Memeland:** confirms and pins RH addresses/constants (router, WETH, USDG), and shows a
  router-guard + RPC health/fallback pattern worth mirroring. The sniper itself is basic.
- **Verdict:** **Study/Adapt** the constant set, router-guard and RPC health/fallback patterns;
  **Skip** wholesale (plaintext key storage, web3.js, no edge).


## Batch 10 - meme-smart-money & agents deep-read (subagent Confucius)

### SRC-040 1009682175845693/bsc-fourmeme-bot (TypeScript, ~17 files)
- **What it is:** Modular CLI toolkit for the Four.meme fork on BNB Chain: sniper, copy-trader, bundler, and a volume (wash-trade) bot. Ethers v6 + PancakeSwap V2 router, commander CLI, pino logging, Telegram notifier. Deliberately on-chain only, no third-party market-data service.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** `getAmountsOut` -> minOut slippage calc (BigInt bips) pattern used consistently; fee-on-transfer-aware sell via `swapExactTokensForETHSupportingFeeOnTransferTokens`; `--dry-run` flag threaded through every module (simulate without sending); thin module classes sharing wallet/provider/logger context; Telegram notifier that no-ops without tokens; hard-coded safe default RPC/router/WBNB addresses; `.npmrc` disabling audit/fund noise.
- **Security/complexity flags:** Env-only private key with essentially no validation (`validator.verifyConfig` is not a real validator API and would throw at runtime); PRIVATE_KEY required even for dry-run; copy-trader is naive (detects any router tx from target then mirrors into a FIXED `defaultToken` instead of decoding the actual token path, ~7% slippage fixed); volume bot is a rate-limited buy/sell wash-trading loop (integrity problem for Memeland); bundler is half-implemented (only `kind: buy`); no tests; heavy `any` typing. README leaks a (placeholder) Telegram bot token in the doc text.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Edge = small, on-chain-only execution path with zero GMGN/data-provider dependency, which maps almost 1:1 onto RH Chain (native ETH + a UniswapV2-style router). The slippage/deadline and fee-on-transfer sell patterns and the dry-run flag design are portable to Memeland's gated executor for RH/BSC. The copy-trade logic is too naive to copy; discovery is manual (hard-coded token lists), no real edge for opportunity detection. Volume bot is wash trading and out of scope.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (execution/slippage + fee-on-transfer sell helpers, dry-run pattern for RH/BSC executor) + **Skip** (volume wash bot, naive copy-trader, incomplete bundler).

### SRC-051 alexskin/memeoy (TypeScript/Next.js, ~200 files)
- **What it is:** Full AI-watched Solana memecoin paper-trading bot + Next.js dashboard. Watches Raydium/PumpSwap pool creation on-chain, runs a chain of fail-closed safety filters, deterministic momentum/revival gates against live DexScreener, then an optional Anthropic "degen score" + final buy/skip judgment (fail-closed to skip). Paper mode by default with a realistic fill simulator; live Raydium swaps only when explicitly enabled. Includes a self-tuning agent and wallet alerts.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (1) Venue-agnostic `PriceSource` + paper fill simulator (`fillSimulator.ts`): quote at decision -> sample modeled latency -> re-quote at slippage 0 -> compare to minAmountOut floor -> mark `reverted_slippage` and retry, charging fees even on failed attempts; `totalFeesPaid` accounts failed retries. (2) Deterministic self-tuning heuristic agent (`heuristicTuner.ts`): fixed whitelist `BOUNDS`, clamp steps, guardrails (never loosens risk-only knobs), LLM only annotates/vetoes; feeds recent-performance + missed-runner-calibration context into the judge prompt. (3) Fail-closed safety filters on RugCheck/DexScreener data (holder concentration, dev holding + top-10 aggregate, insider wallets, burn, fresh-wallet, mutable) - all reject on missing data. (4) Wallet-reputation signal built for free from already-persisted top-holder snapshots correlated to confirmed runners (no extra RPC cost). (5) Global FIFO DexScreener rate limiter (55/min), per-call RPC failover on rate-limit-shaped errors, WS-liveness watchdog with circuit breaker. (6) Pure bigint bonding-curve math separated from I/O. (7) `WalletLogsListener`: onLogs WS push -> getParsedTransaction -> pre/post balance delta = buy detection.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No hardcoded secrets found (all env-driven); `WALLET_PRIVATE_KEY` read only at live-swap signing, never logged; paper-by-default is genuine. Helius key is auto-extracted from the RPC URL query param (minor smell). The LLM decision layer makes "buy" impossible without a key (fail-closed, good). Complexity is high for Memeland's "practical personal deployment" bar if adopted wholesale: Next.js dashboard + worker + SQLite/Turso sync + Helius + several heavy Solana SDKs; ~200 files. LLM per-candidate degen scoring is cost-heavy.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Directly validates the deterministic-gates-first / LLM-explains-survivors shape Memeland already uses, but adds concrete, portable extras: a realistic paper fill simulator (latency + slippage-revert + fee accounting) that sharpens dry-run realism (edge #4), and a deterministic, bounded self-tuning agent that maps onto Memeland's swarm-learning weights (edge #6). Momentum/revival pure evaluators and wallet-reputation-from-holder-snapshots port to EVM chains via DexScreener (provider-independent of GMGN - edge #7). Solana/Raydium plumbing is only for the Solana feed.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (paper fill simulator, bounded self-tuning tuner, fail-closed filter chain, momentum/revival evaluators, wallet-reputation-from-snapshots; each into existing shared services/voters) + **Study** (LLM degen-score vibe check as advisory sentiment input) + **Skip** (Next.js dashboard + Turso public-mirror + whole-platform wholesale - heavy for personal use).

### SRC-061 BikeTysonDegen/gmgn-terminal-bot (C#/.NET WPF, ~60 files incl. tests)
- **What it is:** Windows desktop (WPF) Solana memecoin copy-trading terminal driven entirely by unofficial GMGN public API endpoints (rank, wallet_activity, token_info, trades). Polls selected "leader" wallets, filters, sizes buys (fixed SOL or leader-size x multiplier), sells fraction proportional to a tracked leader bag, with a paper broker (no chain interaction in v0.1). Ships an offline feed that replays recorded mints so the whole loop is testable without network.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** Clean interface split `ITradeSource`/`IPriceSource`/`ITradeExecutor` with `GmgnApiClient` implementing both data interfaces and `PaperBroker` implementing execution - engine depends on abstractions, not concrete clients. Per-leader poll loop with watermark + bounded dedupe (`DedupeCap`), staggered start (400ms), lazy watermark init for mid-run leader adds. Leader-bag tracking keyed `(leader, mint)` with cumulative buy/sell; sell fraction = sold / tracked bag, unknown pre-start bag treated as full exit - a practical copy-timing/sizing heuristic. Pure mapper functions (`GmgnMapper`) tested against recorded JSON fixtures. Proxy URL credentials stripped from logs. Offline feed replays real recorded data for full-loop testing.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No hardcoded secrets found; `PrivateKey` is only a stub for a future live executor and is stored locally in `config.json` (plaintext key at rest - flagged in-app but still plaintext). GMGN access is unofficial: most endpoints sit behind Cloudflare browser-TLS checks, 403 on datacenter IPs, needs residential proxy; client gracefully logs + falls back to offline feed. Solana/GMGN-specific only; a WPF app has no value for Memeland's TUI/Discord/Telegram/REST surfaces. PaperBroker uses a flat placeholder fee, not realistic curve+priority fees.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** The leader-bag sell-fraction sizing (proportional trim, unknown-bag full-exit) is a genuinely reusable copy-trade sizing/timing heuristic for the whale/copy path (edge #3). The watermark/dedupe polling loop and source/executor interface split are clean, portable patterns for any feed (GMGN adapter, RH RPC tape). Offline-feed replay is a useful dry-run/testing idea. Not portable as-is because it is GMGN-unofficial + Solana + Cloudflare-fragile; GMGN ranking/flow already covered by Memeland's shared adapter.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (leader-bag sell-fraction tracking, watermark/dedupe poll loop, source/executor interface split, offline-replay test pattern) + **Skip** (GMGN-unofficial coupling, Cloudflare fragility, plaintext key-at-rest, WPF app, whole-platform).

### SRC-071 chainstacklabs/pumpfun-bonkfun-bot (Python 3.11, ~120 files incl. IDLs/learning-examples)
- **What it is:** Chainstack's Solana sniper/trading bot for pump.fun and letsbonk.fun. Multi-listener architecture (`geyser` Triton gRPC, `logs` logsSubscribe, `blocks` blockSubscribe, `pumpportal` aggregator), platform abstraction (pumpfun/letsbonk), layered trading/position/cleanup/priority-fee modules, YAML per-bot config with validation. Self-flag: "NOT FOR PRODUCTION", learning-focused with standalone `learning-examples/` scripts covering each flow piece.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** "Extreme fast mode" builds a buy entirely from the on-chain CreateEvent (canonical creator, flags, quote mint) so no RPC call happens between detection and buy; the `pumpportal` path does one batched `getMultipleAccounts` and skips if the curve isn't readable within a budget (a guessed account reverts and still costs fees). RPC deadline as a separate wall-clock budget alongside retry counts; treats a `null` `getTransaction` result as "not caught up", not "failed" (load-balanced node distinction that prevents sold-but-unsold confusion). `TradeResult.failure_reason` distinguishes causes so the caller can decide whether resubmission is safe; missing reason defaults to "unknown", never "reverted". Token-bucket RPC rate limiter (burst + refill). Dynamic priority fee via `getRecentPrioritizationFees` median, with fixed-fee fallback. Position dataclass (TP/SL/max-hold). WS max-frame size raised to 32 MiB (Solana subscribe frames exceed the 1 MiB websockets default). Config validation rules with numeric bounds.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No hardcoded secrets found; private key env-driven, keypair loaded at start. Complexity is high for personal use: geyser proto/gRPC, four listeners, platform factory, IDL parsing, multiple IDL JSONs; Python 3.11 + solana/solders/grpcio. Solana-only; author keeps a scam-alert and a not-for-production banner. Learning examples are standalone and do not sign.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** The failure/cause classification (unknown vs reverted vs confirmed) and the "null result is not failure" RPC catch-up handling are directly portable and valuable for fill reliability (edge #4) in any chain adapter. Token-bucket rate limiting and dynamic priority-fee-using-median fit Memeland's RPC failover/rate-limit services. The zero-RPC create-event buy is a sharp speed edge for the Solana feed but irrelevant to RH chain (EVM logs/eth_call path differs). No GMGN dependency (chain + pumpportal aggregator), so it supports provider independence, but it adds no RH/BSC coverage.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (RPC deadline + null-result fill handling, `TradeResult.failure_reason`, token-bucket limiter, dynamic priority/fee median for the Solana executor) + **Study** (extreme-fast zero-RPC event-driven buy as a Solana-feed concept; platform/listener abstraction shape) + **Skip** (wholesale - Python, heavy, Solana-only, not-for-production).
### SRC-072 chasepal/gmgn-wallet-holdings (JS/Chrome extension, ~17 files)
- **What it is:** Single-purpose Chrome extension: add a watchlist of public EVM/Solana wallet addresses, then on any GMGN token page it batch-queries each chain's public RPC (eth_call totalSupply + decimals + balanceOf, or Solana getTokenSupply + getTokenAccountsByOwner) and shows who holds the current token + % of supply, plus a FOMO `Holders (N)` aggregate sync. No private keys, no trading.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:**
  - `lib/holdings.js`: pure, ABI-less ERC-20 helpers — `encodeBalanceOf` (selector + 64-char padded address), `hexToBigInt`, `unitsToDecimal(18-decimals→plain)`, `percentageOf` (6-decimal fixed-point) — directly portable.
  - Batch JSON-RPC with numeric per-call ids, dedupe via `byId` map, per-item `item.error` surfacing, and `withRpcFallback` across an ordered public-RPC list. One round-trip for totalSupply+decimals+N balances.
  - `lib/chains.js`: clean chain-family model (`{id, aliases, family evm|solana, rpcUrls}`), alias→chain resolution, GMGN token-URL parser, address regex validation (EVM / Solana base58). **Includes Robinhood chain 4663 RPCs** (`robinhood-rpc.publicnode.com`, `rpc.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com`) alongside BSC/ETH/Base/Arb/etc.
  - Background cache: TTL (12s) + fixed-cap (200) LRU-dropped map + in-flight promise dedupe so concurrent page mutations don't double-RPC, and cache-clear on watchlist change.
  - Content layer: shadow-DOM dock UI, one MutationObserver + navigation/hash/focus re-schedule, no polling.
  - FOMO session bridge (`lib/fomo-session.js`): in-memory `Authorization` capture via `webRequest`, JWT `exp`-bounded expiry, 429 retry-after cooldown, timed fetch, batched fetch. Good security hygiene (permission assertion is unit-tested).
- **Security/complexity flags:** Minimal permission surface (`storage`, `webRequest`) asserted in tests; sender-origin trust check before serving FOMO data; bearer stored only in memory, never on disk. Public RPCs see your wallet+token queries (documented tradeoff). Body logic has a decent footnote: it only reads balances; doesn't do hold-duration tracking by itself (that must be layered on).
- **For Memeland / edge check:** The multi-chain batch `balanceOf`/holdings reader + chain-family config is exactly the "portfolio positioning / whale watching" primitive Memeland needs, already covering Robinhood 4663 and BSC with fallback RPCs — portable to the TS bot without a framework. Reuse the cache+inFlight pattern to avoid hammering RPC while polling. The chrome/FOMO parts are not applicable.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (port `lib/holdings.js` batch-RPC + `lib/chains.js` chain/alias config + cache/in-flight dedupe as a local holdings watcher; add hold-duration/positioning on top) + **Skip** (extension UI, FOMO session bridge wholesale).
### SRC-078 dartkomnitibe/solana-meme-tool (marketing README only, 1 file)
- **What it is:** A GitHub landing page (only `readme.md` on default branch `main`; codeload tarball = 6.8 KB) describing a closed-source "operator dashboard" for Solana meme-coin launches: pump.fun create + up to 15 bundled sniper wallets in one Jito/Astralane shot, Raydium pool sniper over Yellowstone gRPC, copy-trading with wallet risk scoring, market-cap-triggered limit orders, volume-boost plans, and a token control center (generate/distribute/gather burner wallets). No package.json, no src, no tests are published.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (from README only, unverifiable) Coordinated launch+bundle-in-one-tx, LP-burn/renounced-mint/mutable-metadata toggle gates before entry, feature-level wallet risk scoring (tx rate, fail %, unique peers, fee/dust patterns), "thin encrypted API route → service → RPC/Mongo/Redis" layering, AES-encrypt keys before they leave browser.
- **Security/complexity flags:** Heavy stack (Next.js 14 + Mongo + Redis + Yellowstone gRPC + Jito + BirdEye + GMGN/TradingView), 8 GB heap, 16 API routes — far from a lightweight edge. Risk signals above are described, not present. Keys "AES-encrypted in browser" but secret hygiene depends on user infra; no code to audit for real hardcoded keys.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Zero code = zero portable primitives; the only useful takeaways are conceptual (check LP-burn/renounce before entry; feature-based wallet risk scoring). Green-label bar is about real, simple, auditable code — this provides none.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** — repo publishes no source code, only a sales README; nothing to deep-read or port.
### SRC-081 Denzz102/memecoin-agent (Node.js/Express+MySQL, ~25 files)
- **What it is:** AI-assisted Solana memecoin screener. A cron scan pulls "newest" tokens from DexScreener `token-profiles/latest/v1`, then for each: filters (min $5k liquidity), analyzes top-holder concentration + top-holder addresses via Helius RPC (`getTokenSupply` + `getTokenLargestAccounts`), counts "smart wallet" participation (intersection with a curated `smart_wallets` table), crude whale heuristics on h24 volume/buys, checks RugCheck, computes a deterministic score → BUY/WATCH/AVOID, then has Groq LLM write a ≤50-word narrative and alerts Telegram. Persists tokens/history/portfolio in MySQL; has an Express+socket.io dashboard and health monitor. Does NOT trade or touch private keys.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:**
  - `services/scoring.js` — clean deterministic weighted scoring, no LLM in the decision loop: liquidity tiers (+5/+10/+15), volume tiers, mcap band (+15 at $25k–$250k), top-holder concentration (≤5% +25 … ≥40% −10), buy/sell ratio (≥3x +20), smart-wallet count, whale count, RugCheck (SAFE +10 / RISK −50). Clamped 0–100; BUY ≥70, WATCH ≥60, else AVOID. Directly portable signal logic.
  - `services/holderAnalyzer.js` — top-holder % and top-holder address list from RPC largest-accounts; feeds both concentration score and smart-wallet intersection.
  - `services/smartWallet.js` + `signalTracker.js` — join top holders against a tracked smart-wallet set; dedupe alerts with a 6-hour window (fail-safe against spam).
  - Discovery via free DexScreener `token-profiles/latest/v1` (no API key) — a usable new-meme seed feed.
  - AI is strictly garnish: `analyzeToken` only summarizes already-decided signals; core scoring stays deterministic and testable. Optional (Groq/OpenRouter interchangeable).
  - Portfolio tracker (entry/current/ATH mcap, gain %) updated idempotently in SQL.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No private-key handling at all (screener-only) — strong default. Helius key passed in URL query string (`?api-key=`) risks log/proxy leakage (prefer Authorization header). No hardcoded secrets found (all via `.env`). Errors swallowed to fail-open defaults (e.g. holder=100 on failure lowers score → effectively fail-safe toward AVOID). Moderate stack: Express+MySQL+socket.io+Telegram; `config/gmgn.js` is misnamed (it's Helius config) — actual GMGN coupling is zero.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** The deterministic scoring model (holder concentration, buy-ratio, smart-wallet participation, gate checks) and DexScreener discovery are real, simple, and portable. Caveat: Helius and RugCheck are Solana-only; for Robinhood/BSC, largest-holder concentration can come from generic ERC-20 balance queries and rug checks from DexScreener/other. "Whale" here is just h24 volume/buy thresholds — not true whale detection.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (port the weighted scoring model + smart-wallet-participation + holder-concentration edge; keep AI narrative optional) + **Study** (whale heuristic is crude — replace with real on-chain whale detection) + **Skip** (MySQL+Express+dashboard+Telegram stack wholesale).
### SRC-086 ejfxgit2025/memenet (TypeScript, React/Vite + Express + Supabase, ~40 files)
- **What it is:** "MemeNet" — a memecoin social-intelligence platform: users submit tokens, a scheduler runs an event engine that fuses CoinGecko (price/vol/mcap), DexScreener (pair liquidity/5m-1h price), Twitter v2 (tweet count + sentiment) and Firecrawl (news headlines) into structured "signals", AI (OpenRouter/Genai) enriches them into posts, all stored in Supabase and shown in a live feed. No trading, no keys.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:**
  - `signalEngine.ts` — a **fail-closed deterministic signal classifier**: strict 5-signal taxonomy (whale_activity / liquidity_event / news_event / social_spike / market_movement), `classifySignal()` returns `no_signal` when data is weak (rule: "NEVER invent information"), `scoreSignal()` = base + magnitude bonuses (price>5/10/20%, volume tiers, 5m flash moves, liquidity depth, engagement, sentiment) + cross-source bonus (≥2/3 sources) clamped 0–100, and an `antiSpam()` forbidden-phrase gate. Token-specific priority profiles (BONK→social, CAKE→liquidity…). Exactly Memeland's "fail-closed" ethos.
  - Threading + dedup: 15-min thread-window grouping (`generateThreadId`) and 60s per-token dedup before writing events — controls alert spam.
  - `dataEngine.ts` — clean multi-source adapter pattern: TTL in-memory cache per source (CoinGecko 60s, DexScreener 90s, Twitter 300s, Firecrawl 600s) to stay under free-tier rate limits; `Promise.allSettled` so one source failing never aborts the cycle; canonical pair = highest-liquidity DexScreener pair; naive keyword-based sentiment (POSITIVE_WORDS/NEGATIVE_WORDS → normalized ±1, no NLP).
  - `costControl.ts` — daily LLM budget ($2/day), frugal mode at 80% usage forces cheapest model, per-task maxInput/maxOutput token caps. Good guardrail if Memeland ever uses LLM.
  - `validationEngine.ts` (blocked-term filter) + `parseCoinGeckoId.ts` / `parseDexScreenerUrl.ts` (robust URL→id/address parsers for CoinGecko/DexScreener).
- **Security/complexity flags:** All keys via `.env` (Supabase anon, OpenRouter, Firecrawl, Twitter bearer) — no hardcoded secrets. Twitter & Firecrawl are paid/gated (Twitter off by default via `ENABLE_TWITTER`). Heavy stack (React + Supabase + Firecrawl + OpenRouter). `whale_activity` is a crude `volume > 0.5×liquidity` heuristic, not real whale tracking; sentiment is keyword-counting. No private-key handling anywhere (platform is read-only data).
- **For Memeland / edge check:** The deterministic, fail-closed signal classifier + multi-source fusion with cross-source bonuses and free-tier TTL caching are portable and align with the green label. Cost-control and anti-spam gates are small, adaptable guardrails. The social/news/Twitter/Firecrawl channels are expensive, GMGN-unrelated noise for an execution edge; real whale detection must come from elsewhere.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (port the fail-closed `classifySignal`/`scoreSignal`/`antiSpam` signal model + multi-source fusion with `Promise.allSettled` + TTL caching for free DexScreener/CoinGecko + `costControl` guardrail) + **Skip** (React/Supabase social feed and paid Firecrawl/Twitter news channels wholesale).
### SRC-087 ennriqe/crypto-agent-memecoin-prototype (Python, hackathon, ~3 code files + ML artifacts)
- **What it is:** ETHGlobal Bangkok 2024 hackathon prototype with two tracks: (1) an LSTM price-prediction pipeline (`crypto_price_prediction.py`) trained on 15-min OHLCV sequences across ~230 Solana/Base meme coins from The Graph subgraphs, and (2) a LangGraph + Coinbase CDP AgentKit agent (`my_bot.py`) that runs on Base mainnet, uses an LLM to generate pandas filter queries over a CoinGecko-derived meme-coin dataframe ("RAG"-style), fetches candle data per contract, predicts next 15-min move, and offers to buy via CDP tools (`wow_buy_token` deliberately removed).
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:**
  - `get_candlestick_chart_data()` in `my_bot.py` — **subgraph swap→OHLCV reconstruction**: GraphQL queries a Uniswap-style subgraph for a token's pools against WETH, paginates Swap events, and builds 15-min open/high/low/close/volume bars using volume-weighted token/WETH ratios, with `ThreadPoolExecutor` parallel intervals + rate-limit sleeps. This is a deterministic, chain-agnostic way to reconstruct local price/volume history without any proprietary API — genuinely portable to Robinhood/BSC via their subgraphs.
  - `predict_price()` — MinMaxScaler-normalized 30-step sequence fed to a 2-layer LSTM (128→64) + Dense predicting next close; shows a prediction/reality scatter overlay. Research-grade, unvalidated as an edge.
  - `get_RAG_query()` — LLM emits a pandas `.query()` string filtered by boolean category columns (cat_themed, ai_meme, politifi…) to slice coins by theme; interesting cheap "semantic filter" pattern but fragile (raw pandas eval of LLM output).
  - Agent orchestration pattern: LangGraph ReAct agent + CDP toolkit on Base, chat/autonomous modes.
- **Security/complexity flags:** CDP MPC wallet export handled (`agentkit.export_wallet()`) with commented-out disk persistence — if enabled, a private wallet file lands on disk. Autonomous mode ("do something interesting on the blockchain") with a live wallet is risky. Missing env keys only print a warning (not fail-closed). Massive dependency footprint (TensorFlow/Keras/LangChain/LangGraph/CDP/Web3). Hardcoded Base WETH contract. LLM-generated pandas query is evaluated with `exec`-adjacent semantics — injection-prone. Not a bot you run unattended.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** The subgraph swap→OHLCV reconstruction is the standout portable primitive — deterministic local price/volume history per chain, no GMGN/proprietary dependency. The LSTM predictor is heavy ML with dubious edge for short-horizon meme moves; treat as Study only. The CDP/LangGraph execution stack is Base-specific and too heavy to port.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (port the subgraph swap→OHLCV local candle-builder as a deterministic on-chain data primitive) + **Study** (LSTM sequence prediction as a research experiment, low-confidence edge) + **Skip** (CDP AgentKit execution agent + TensorFlow stack wholesale).
### SRC-130 kukapay/pumpfun-wallets-mcp (Python MCP server, ~1 code file)
- **What it is:** A minimal MCP server exposing 4 read-only tools that wrap hardcoded **Dune Analytics** query IDs to report Pump.fun/PumpSwap wallet stats: total wallets, top profitable wallets (30d realized profit), top trading wallets (all-time volume), and wallet distribution by volume tier. Tabulated-string output. All analysis lives in external Dune SQL; the repo contains no query logic.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** The thin-MCP-tool pattern (FastMCP `@mcp.tool()` decorators + a `get_latest_result(query_id, limit)` helper that fetches Dune query results with a 300s timeout). Not self-contained — you cannot tell what the "alpha wallet" logic actually is without the Dune dashboards (query IDs 5239155/4032586/5232018/5239138).
- **Security/complexity flags:** DUNE_API_KEY from env, no hardcoded keys. Broad `except: return 0/""` swallows all errors (read-only so low blast radius, but masks failures). No input validation; `limit` passed straight to Dune. Read-only — touches no funds. Opaque external dependency (Dune Analytics, which needs a paid/tiered API key).
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Not an edge — it is a passthrough to a third-party analytics platform, and the queries are Pump.fun/Solana-specific (not portable to Robinhood/BSC). GMGN-independent but swaps one platform dependency for another (Dune). The MCP-wrapper pattern is only useful if Memeland wants to expose its own local analytics as tools.
- **Verdict:** **Skip** — thin proxy over opaque Dune SQL, platform-locked to Pump.fun, no self-contained/portable edge. (Pattern note only: expose self-computed analytics via a thin tool layer rather than proxying someone else's.)
### SRC-132 kukapay/whale-tracker-mcp (Python MCP server, ~1 code file)
- **What it is:** A minimal MCP server that wraps the **Whale Alert API** to surface large cryptocurrency transfers: two tools (`get_recent_transactions`, `get_transaction_details`), a dynamic resource (`whale://transactions/{blockchain}`), and a prompt template. Async httpx calls; key from env. Read-only; no analysis beyond echoing raw API rows.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** The lean FastMCP server skeleton (tool/resource/prompt + single async `fetch_from_api` helper + `load_dotenv` key loading). Useful as a structural template for exposing read-only data tools, but the actual signal source is an external API.
- **Security/complexity flags:** `raise ValueError` if key missing (good fail-fast). `WHALE_ALERT_API_KEY` from env, not hardcoded. No input bounds on `limit`/`min_value`. Read-only, no key handling.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Whale Alert tracks large transfers on major L1s (BTC/ETH and select chains) between known entities — it does not cover pump.fun/4Meme DEX-level meme wallets on Robinhood/Solana/BSC, which is Memeland's actual edge. So the data source is largely irrelevant to Memeland; only the thin MCP-server pattern is portable, and Memeland already gets whale data better from on-chain RPC (see SRC-072).
- **Verdict:** **Skip** — thin proxy over a whale data source that doesn't cover meme DEX chains; no self-contained or portable edge.
### SRC-147 MayurK-cmd/4Meme-Pilot (Python agent + Node backend + React frontend, ~60 files)
- **What it is:** "4MemePilot" — an autonomous 5-min-cycle trading bot for BSC's 4.meme launchpad (bonding-curve memecoins). Python agent (`agent/`) scans trending/new/watchlist tokens, computes bonding-curve stage + holder-concentration rug flags, feeds Gemini 2.5 Flash a big signal context, then BUY/SELL/HOLDs via 4.meme TokenManager contracts. Ships a Node/Express+Mongo backend (Privy auth, AES wallet store) and a React dashboard. Dry-run default on; hybrid "testnet logging / mainnet trading".
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (1) `market.py` — clean deterministic signal layer: bonding-curve stage thresholds (0-30 early / 30-70 momentum / 70-95 late / near-graduation), holder-concentration bands (<40 LOW / 40-60 MED / >60 HIGH), buy/sell pressure ratio (>3:1 strong buy), launch-age risk tiers, liquidity tiers, and a composite `rug_risk_score` (0-100: conc 40 + age 30 + liq 20 + stage 10). (2) `four_meme.py` — complete 4.meme integration: REST helpers `private/token/get/v2` + `public/token/search` with exponential-backoff retries, on-chain `TokenManagerHelper3.getTokenInfo` (V1/V2 detection, bonding fill from offers/maxOffers, funds raised), pre-trade estimates via `tryBuy`/`trySell` static calls, V1/V2 buy/sell (`purchaseTokenAMAP`/`buyTokenAMAP`, `saleToken`/`sellToken`) with slippage-protected min amounts + ERC20 approve, and a 60s BNB price cache. (3) `strategy.py` — deterministic fallback scoring engine (additive buy_score/sell_score with decision threshold, confidence = 0.5 + score delta, size buckets 0.25/0.5/0.75) used when Gemini is absent/fails; fail-closed HOLD on insufficient balance. (4) `main.py` — per-token ThreadPool (≤5) cycle, backend-config fallback to `.env`, max-positions and min-confidence gates before any order. (5) Per-position SL/TP percentage exits (`should_exit`) and JSON persistence of open positions.
- **Security/complexity flags:** ⚠️ **Hardcoded agent secret** — `AGENT_API_SECRET=aa78d2bdf36c0144bbf31ff05450d8d183f144b03a76a65e811e18af47d83480` is committed in BOTH `agent/.env.example` and `agent/.claude/settings.local.json` (concrete value, not a placeholder). That secret gates the backend's `GET /api/agent/wallet`, which returns the **decrypted BSC private key**; the route also grabs the *first onboarded user's* wallet (single-key shared model). If deployed as-is, anyone with the leaked secret can pull the live wallet key. AES uses sha256(secret) with a default-empty `ENCRYPTION_SECRET` (fail-open to ""). Root `.env.agent` is committed (only placeholders, but references the real Pacifica prod URLs). `agent/.gitignore` only ignores `.env`, so `positions.json`/`trades.json` (wallet addresses, entry prices) are tracked. Per-token execute in threads but position store is a plain dict written without a lock — race on concurrent threads. Complexity is high (agent+backend+frontend).
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Strong **green-label fit for the bonding-curve + rug-gate framework**: the deterministic signal thresholds, composite rug_risk_score, and "momentum phase 30-70% + conc <40% + buy/sell >3:1" confluence are exactly the kind of local, chain-agnostic filters Memeland needs, and the thresholds transfer directly to Solana pump.fun and BSC 4.meme. The 4.meme TokenManager V1/V2/Helper3 buy/sell + tryBuy/trySell estimate wrappers are directly reusable for Memeland's BSC leg. But the Gemini decision engine, Elfa AI dependency, and full backend/frontend are heavyweight and not green — replace the LLM with the built-in deterministic scorer. DRY_RUN default and fail-closed HOLD on low balance are patterns worth copying.
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (bonding-curve stage framework, rug_risk composite, fail-closed deterministic scoring, slippage-protected buy/sell + on-chain estimates for the BSC 4.meme leg) **+ Skip** (Gemini-LLM loop, Elfa sentiment, full-stack dashboard, and especially the shared-backend wallet-credential model with its leaked hardcoded secret — keep keys local-only).
### SRC-162 nhovongoc0-max/meme-radar (JavaScript/ESM Node app + local web UI, ~60 files incl. 20 tests)
- **What it is:** "Meme Radar 开源版" — a local-only, **read-only** multi-chain meme-token research radar (no wallet keys, no signing, no trading). Discovers tokens via `gmgn-cli@1.5.7` (sole dependency), risk-verifies with GoPlus and DexScreener where supported, and shows evidence for human review. Supports 7 chains incl. **Robinhood Chain** (defaults to `chain:'robinhood'` in config). Pushes candidates through discovery screen → deep audit → X_REVIEW; tracks 5m-24h "shadow performance" of passed/rejected groups. Strong Chinese UX, voice alerts, Dockerless local app.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (1) **Fail-closed scoring** (`scoring.mjs`) — every risk field carries an "unknown" state; `deepScreen` treats missing data as a *blocking* unknown that forces `WAITING`/no-pass rather than assuming safe ("未知字段不假装通过"). (2) Rigorous signal normalization: `optionalRate/optionalNumber/optionalBoolean` never guess units, addresses validated per-chain (Solana base58 vs EVM 0x), tags normalized. (3) **`analyzeWallets`** — dedupes holder rows by address, classifies ordinary vs risk-tagged (bundler/rat/sniper/wash), computes `botHoldRate`, `linkedHoldRate` (wallets sharing a funding source), ordinary-wallet sample count, with `dataComplete` gate. (4) **`marketBehaviorScreen`** — conservative *downgrade* signals (not fraud claims): KOL-only (1 smart + 1 KOL, no 2nd smart), swap/holder mismatch, distribution-flow (price up + sell pressure), old-sudden-pump, fading-pump, repeat-launcher-with-weak-history. (5) **`observeFiveMinutes` + `chartRiskScreen`** — local 1m-candle window analysis: vertical+plateau pump pattern (≥35% jump then ≤15% plateau) and confirmed-peak drawdown exclusion, with continuity/freshness/duplicate-bar validation. (6) **`empiricalSellability`** — sells_5m/sells_24h + distinct active sellers as a sell-side proxy for EVM chains where honeypot status is unknown. (7) **GMGN client** (`gmgn.mjs`) — request-weight-based token-bucket (`minRequestGapMs*weight*backoff`), TTL LRU cache (500), `Promise.allSettled` static→dynamic two-stage audit, retry-after parse, backoff that decays on success streaks, `--raw` bounded worker. (8) **Secret handling** (`gmgn-key-store.mjs`) — key dir `0700`/files `0600`, symlink checks, API key + Ed25519 signing key kept only on local disk, not in env/logs/HTTP; key stored as `gmgn_` suffix-validated. (9) config: bounded integer env parsing with safe fallbacks.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No hardcoded secrets found; key-store is genuinely careful (restrictive perms, symlink guard, never returns private key to the browser). `gitignore` excludes state. Risk fields are handled conservatively (missing ≠ safe) — this is the project's strongest trait. Main concern: **entirely GMGN-coupled** — every data source (discovery, security, holders, traders, candles) goes through `gmgn-cli`; GoPlus/DexScreener only supplement. It's read-only (no trading) so no money at risk, but a GMGN outage or API-key tightening kills all discovery. Fairly heavy: a local server + frontend dashboard + packaging + supervisor + ~20 test files for what is essentially a scanner; some mojibake-named `.bat` launcher files (benign — just node checks + open.mjs). AGPL-3.0 license (ignored per personal-use rule).
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Strong conceptual match for Memeland's fail-closed green-label ethos: the unknown-field tracking, wallet-classification (ordinary vs bot/bundler/linked), downgrade-signal philosophy, 1m-candle chart-risk pattern, and empirical sellability proxy are exactly the kind of deterministic, chain-agnostic filters Memeland wants. The multi-chain config (incl. Robinhood 4663) and request-weight/backoff client also generalize. BUT it is **wholly GMGN-dependent** (fails Memeland's "not too GMGN-dependent" bar) and is read-only — no execution, SL/TP, or portfolio. The *architecture* is portable even though the data layer is not.
- **Verdict:** **Study** (reusable fail-closed risk-filter + wallet-classification + chart-risk + sellability patterns, and the per-chain/request-budget design — port the *scoring architecture*, not the GMGN data layer) **+ Skip** (GMGN-only coupling conflicts with the green-label bar, no trade execution, heavy local-app/dashboard package).
### SRC-187 rckprtr/pumpdotfun-sdk (TypeScript library, ~20 files incl. ESM/CJS/browser builds + tests + examples)
- **What it is:** The canonical lightweight TypeScript SDK for interacting with the **Pump.fun** Solana bonding-curve launchpad. Non-custodial library: creates, buys, sells tokens by building Anchor program transactions. Ships pure arithmetic for the bonding curve, an AMM simulator, program event parsing, and slippage-aware buy/sell. ESM + CJS + browser bundles. MIT, deps only `@coral-xyz/anchor`, `@solana/spl-token`, `@solana/web3.js`, `@coral-xyz/borsh`.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (1) **`bondingCurveAccount.ts`** — pure bonding-curve math on the on-chain account: `getBuyPrice`, `getSellPrice(amount, feeBasisPoints)`, `getMarketCapSOL`, `getFinalMarketCapSOL`, `getBuyOutPrice`, plus exact Borsh decode of the on-chain account (virtual/real reserves, supply, complete flag). This is the *deterministic price model* for the whole curve. (2) **`amm.ts`** — a pure in-memory AMM simulator (`getBuyPrice/applyBuy/applySell`) reconstructable from a `GlobalAccount` or `BondingCurveAccount` — perfect for offline/paper simulation before going live. (3) **`util.ts`** — slippage helpers (buy = amount + (amount*bps)/10000 = max SOL to spend; sell = amount - ... = min SOL to receive), and `sendTx` that builds a versioned tx with optional compute-unit priority fees, `skipPreflight:false`, then confirms and returns full tx details. (4) **`events.ts` + `types.ts`** — typed parsing of `create/trade/complete/setParams` events (mint, reserves, isBuy) — a clean basis for a wallet/whale tracker or position ledger. (5) `pumpfun.ts` — program ID + seeds (`global`, `mint-authority`, `bonding-curve`, `metadata`) and `createAndBuy`/`buy`/`sell`/`getBuyInstructions` builders with finality/commitment params.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No hardcoded secrets; caller owns the Keypair (non-custodial). `.env.example` only `HELIUS_RPC_URL`. Uses `skipPreflight:false` (safe) and `finalized` commitment by default. Verified slippage defaults (500 bps). Minimal surface — low complexity, no heavyweight framework. Anchor/IDL dependency is the main coupling; `DEFAULT_COMMITMENT/finality = finalized` is the safe but slow choice. Note the README itself warns not to trust unsolicited code/links (good hygiene).
- **For Memeland / edge check:** **Strong green-label fit for the Solana leg.** The pure bonding-curve pricing (`getBuyPrice/getSellPrice/getMarketCapSOL/getFinalMarketCapSOL`), the AMM simulator for offline/paper backtests, the slippage math, and the trade-event parsing are all small, deterministic, portable algorithms with no GMGN/data-vendor coupling. The instruction-building is pump.fun-specific but the *math* generalizes to any bonding-curve launchpad (pump.fun Solana; conceptually maps to BSC 4.meme's curve too). Library-grade, MIT — ideal to vendor/copy the math + AMM into Memeland's own engine rather than depend on the SDK directly.
- **Verdict:** **Adopt** (vend the bonding-curve math, AMM simulator, slippage and event-parsing into Memeland's Solana engine — pure, simple, no external-data coupling) **+ Adapt** (transaction builders + priority-fee sendTx to match Memeland's RPC/relay setup).
### SRC-189 ricoboost/meme-coin-trading-bot (Python, ~120 files — src pkg + docs + collectors + dashboard)
- **What it is:** A heavyweight Solana memecoin **whale-cluster / copy-trading bot**. Streams Pump.fun / Raydium / Pump-AMM / LaunchLab swaps over **Yellowstone gRPC**, maintains per-token rolling buy clusters, detects candidates when N distinct buyers + tx + volume appear in 30s, feeds them through offline-mined CSV **rule packs** (feature thresholds + regime tag + historical hit-rate), optional **ML qualifier** (off/shadow/gate), rug checks, regime detection, and a multi-stage exit engine with TP fractions, trailing stops, and timeouts. Paper trading default (`ENABLE_AUTO_TRADING=false`); live execution via Helius sender / Jito bundle. README is strikingly honest: "This worked in backtest. It did not work live."
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (1) **`rug_check.py`** — state-free async `RugChecker` (pydantic verdict + flags) over Helius RPC: mint/freeze authority null checks, top-1/top-5 non-pool holder concentration (with pool/burn-owner exclusion), Jupiter quote sanity (route + price-impact), all concurrent with a 60s TTL cache and `dataComplete`-style unknown handling. (2) **`risk_manager.py`** — clean deterministic risk: max position/exposure/open-count, **daily-loss halt** (net realized PnL persisted per-day in SQLite), per-token **cooldowns**, and a **session burned-token blacklist** (never re-enter a token closed at a loss). (3) **`rule_matcher.py`** — **schema-driven, fail-closed rule engine**: condition keys are `{feature}_{min|max}`, validated at load against a `KNOWN_FEATURES` set so a CSV typo never silently produces an unmatchable rule; miss reasons are human-readable and support "closest miss" diagnostics; regime-aware rule selection by hit-rate/support/priority. (4) **`exit_engine.py`** — rule-aware multi-stage exits: TP1/TP2/TP3 with sell fractions, confirm-ticks, trailing drawdown, outlier-price guards (median-window), dead-token/absolute-max-hold timeouts. (5) **Paper round-trip guard** — before entry, quote a buy+sell to verify executable roundtrip ratio / price-impact (per-venue thresholds) so paper entries only fire when liquidity is actually real. (6) `signer.py` — lazy solders keypair, key never logged.
- **Security/complexity flags:** No hardcoded secrets (all in `.env.example`; `BOT_PRIVATE_KEY_B58` from env, signer never logs it; keypad placeholder values like Jito tip accounts in env example are standard). Paper default + opt-in live is a good safety default. **Major complexity**: ~120 files, Yellowstone gRPC protos, Playwright wallet scrapers, sklearn ML, FastAPI dashboard, dozens of tunables — far beyond green-label "simple". Solana-locked (Helius/Jupiter/solders/ATA). ML in `gate` mode can block trades; in `shadow` it's data-collection. The honest "didn't work live" note undercuts the premise.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** Excellent source of **portable deterministic risk/ops patterns**: the state-free rug-check helper, the daily-loss-halt + cooldown + burned-token risk manager, the fail-closed schema-driven rule matcher, the multi-stage exit state machine, and the paper round-trip liquidity guard are all chain-agnostic and directly reusable in Memeland's engine. But the bot *as a whole* is heavy and Solana-exclusive; the cluster/copy-trading premise and ML layer are not green-label (and not validated live).
- **Verdict:** **Adapt** (state-free rug-check helper, deterministic RiskManager with daily-loss halt/cooldowns/burned-token blacklist, fail-closed rule-matcher schema, staged TP/SL exit engine, paper round-trip liquidity guard) **+ Skip** (whole Solana Yellowstone/Helius/Jupiter stack, ML qualifier, Playwright wallet collectors, FastAPI dashboard).
### SRC-200 slightlyuseless/pons-sniper (TypeScript/viem ESM, ~27 source files, tsx + zod + pino + ws)
- **What it is:** Open-source sniper for the pons memecoin factory ON ROBINHOOD CHAIN (chain id 4663) — Memeland's home venue. Monitors v1 (Uniswap v3 pool) and v2 (bonding curve) launches via WS event subscription (HTTP-poll fallback), hard-filters to one target deployer, then enters with per-wallet buy/sell/slippage/take-profit/stop-loss rules. Includes a public-mode "wait out the decaying opening tax" optimiser, an exempt-mode immediate entry that first verifies the recipient's actual tax, multi-wallet batched firing, a PositionManager exit engine, and CLI tools (calibrate, verify, wallets, generate-wallets, autosize, listen, simulate). Purely on-chain, viem/zod, no GMGN/data-provider.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** (1) `quote.ts` — protocol-exact bigint constant-product curve math (`amountOut`/`amountIn`, fee-off-input `quoteBuy` with clamp+refund, `quoteSell` fee-off-output, `applyBuy` to advance state as if siblings bought first, `minTokensOutFromRate` bounding the rate not the quantity). (2) `snipeTax.ts` — power-law decay model `tax(t)=OPEN*(1-t/W)` with `elapsedFromTax`/`projectTax`/`msUntilTax`, anchored on the live on-chain tax read (not local clock) so there is zero clock-skew; `effectiveSnipeBps` caps the raw read. (3) `strategy.ts` — a real economic entry-timing optimiser: compares forward-projected tax relief vs EWMA price drift (`-tax''/(1-tax) > 2*Q''/Q`) every tick, guarded by hard MAX_ENTRY_SNIPE_TAX_BPS + MIN/MAX delay abort bounds; `impactBps` position-size sanity. (4) `exemptSnipe.ts` — never-fire-without-verifying pattern: reads `currentSnipeTaxBps(recipient)` before each exempt buy and aborts if not zero; shares curve state across a batch (4 shared reads + 1 per-recipient) to cut RPC count; bounds every wallet as if it lands LAST (`siblingQuoteIn`) so no sequencer ordering can revert. (5) `executor.ts` — `readFill` from the actual CurveBuy/Swap event (never trusts the quote), `ensureApproval` only when allowance is short, per-wallet `NonceManager` (prime ahead, serialise per wallet, resync on error), writes over plain HTTP with retryCount 0 to avoid nonce ambiguity. (6) `chain.ts` — dual client (batched public for steady state, unbatched `fastClient` for the block-zero race), background-primed cached gas (`primeGasCache`), correct insight that Orbit sequencers are first-come-first-served (no priority-fee auction; races won on latency), explicit `maxPriorityFeePerGas: 0`. (7) `filters.ts` — cheapest-first launch screening (allow/blocklists with no network, then parallel on-chain reads); `Phase` as the authoritative signal; fee caps. (8) `batching.ts`/`positions.ts` — bounded batch firing (start batch concurrently, pause between, wait at end) and a PositionManager with per-position `exitsInFlight` dedupe, graduation-imminent forced exit, near-sold-out curve exit, partial tranche exit bookkeeping. (9) strict zod env validation (safeParse + exit), credentials file validated for dup/type/match, `0o600` chmod on write.
- **Security/complexity flags:** Private keys live in a plaintext `credentials.json` (chmod 0600 on write, validated & matched against publicKey) — file-permission-dependent, standard for such bots but flag it. `.env.example` holds a placeholder Alchemy key only; no real secrets committed. Public RPC fallbacks hard-coded (public endpoints, benign). No tests at all (devDeps are only tsx/types; the tool scripts are manual verification). Code is dense but clean; the v1 exactOutput restricted-window path (`v1.ts`) is pons-specific and the most complex/least portable module. Strategy model constants (window 3s vs 5s, K=6.17) are calibrated to pons and would need re-fitting per venue.
- **For Memeland / edge check:** This is the closest possible match — same chain 4663, same bonding-curve + Uniswap-v3 DEX reality, viem/zod/TS stack, zero GMGN/data-provider, fully deterministic local math. The curve `quote.ts` math, the tax-window timing optimiser (`strategy.ts`/`snipeTax.ts`), the verify-before-exempt-fire and worst-order multi-wallet slippage patterns, and the fill-read-from-event executor are directly portable to Memeland's RH-chain gated executor and could serve as the execution core. Its screening is intentionally thin (single-deployer hard filter, no off-chain quality/edge scoring) — the entry timing/execution edge is real but Memeland still needs its own opportunity-detection/risk layer on top. pons-specific ABI/contracts and the v1 restricted-block path do not transfer as-is.
- **Verdict:** **Adopt** (curve `quote.ts` + `snipeTax.ts` + `strategy.ts` timing optimiser + verify-before-fire + event-read fill executor + nonce manager + gas caching — directly on Memeland's chain, deterministic, zero GMGN, green-label execution core) + **Adapt** (Multi-wallet worst-order batching, batched/fast dual client, position manager exit engine, cheapest-first filters) + **Skip** (pons v1 restricted-block path, target-deployer single-launch focus, no real opportunity edge).
### SRC-201 soladdev/solana-meme-tool (TypeScript/Next.js 14, ~170 files — heavy dashboard + base/ services)
- **What it is:** A commercial-style Solana memecoin "automation stack" dashboard: token launchpad with bundled liquidity, bundle-buy coordinator, Jito sniper, copy-trading bot, limit-order engine, token control center, volume-boosting (wash-trade) suite, wallet risk intelligence, and a market "trenches" feed. Next.js 14 `/pages`, HeroUI, Zustand, MongoDB (orders/tokens/wallets), Redis, Yellowstone gRPC for token/pool streaming, Raydium SDK + Jupiter for swaps, Jito block-engine for bundles, BirdEye/GeckoTerminal/Dexscreener for data.
- **Key reusable pieces / patterns:** The one genuinely portable idea is the `base/wallet-check` risk scaffold — a feature-extraction + bucketed risk-score approach (tx counts, failure rate, recipients, fees, scam interactions, recent-creation, large-transfers) feeding a weighted `calculateRiskScore`. Also a `BufferRingBuffer` for holding recent gRPC account data. Everything else (bundling, Jito, volume) is Solana-specific.
- **Security/complexity flags:** SECURITY — all secrets (MongoDB URL, BirdEye key, Jito UUID, AES `ENCRYPT_KEY`, Astralane key) are declared as `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`, which Next.js bundles into the browser client: keys are effectively public, and the AES key used to "encrypt" wallet private keys in the UI is itself client-exposed (CryptoJS AES in `lib/decrypt.ts`) — obfuscation, not security. Private keys are pasted into the UI and sent to API routes. COMPLEXITY — enormous dep tree (anchor 0.30 + 0.25, two Raydium SDKs, metaplex, jito-ts, mongodb, redis).
