### SRC-024 api-docs - CoinCap v3 API (agent-friendly + x402)
- **What it is:** CoinCap is a free real-time crypto price/market-cap/exchange API for 1000+ top cryptocurrencies. Docs moved to rest.coincap.io with a JSON OpenAPI spec (api-docs.json). New v3 surface includes price by symbol/address, assets/markets/rates, and server-side technical-analysis endpoints (SMA, EMA, MACD, RSI, VWAP, candlesticks), plus an "agentFriendly" namespace (assets_search, full_assets_by_slug, history, ta candlesticks_batch, top_movers, news_top, market-cap history) designed for LLM/agent consumption. Auth via accountless x402 protocol: POST /prepaid/create gives 100 free credits, refilled with USDC on Base; there is also an MCP endpoint (/mcp) and an official MCP server (CC3-0/coincap-mcp-server).
- **Key pattern:** Precomputed indicator endpoints (RSI/MACD/EMA/VWAP/candlesticks) remove client-side math; "agentFriendly" batched endpoints are shaped for AI agents; x402 accountless prepaid keys + MCP exposure are a clean paid-API onboarding pattern. OpenAPI JSON makes codegen trivial.
- **For Memeland:** Data is broad-market/top-1000 centralized, not memecoin DEX pairs, and has no RH-4663 coverage, so it is not a GMGN replacement for Memeland's actual universe. Transferable value is the API shape: exposing batched agent-friendly endpoints and x402/prepaid keyless auth as an onboarding pattern, and MCP hosting. Memeland's engine likely already computes its own indicators, so the TA endpoints are not an edge.
- **Costs:** Free tier (100 credits) then x402 prepaid refills (USDC/Base); a paid-key dependency if used at scale. Low complexity, clean OpenAPI.
- **Verdict:** Study <portable agentFriendly/x402/MCP API design; feed itself irrelevant to memecoin + no RH>
- **Security/complexity flags:** accountless x402 prepaid keys, OpenAPI JSON, MCP exposure; verdict-ready study-as-pattern not live feed.
### SRC-025 api-docs - DexScreener (docs + API reference)
- **What it is:** DexScreener is a keyless, free multi-chain DEX screener API (GitBook docs; api.dexscreener.com). Reference covers pair/token data: /latest/dex/search?q=, /latest/dex/pairs/{chainId}/{pairId}, /token-pairs/v1/{chainId}/{tokenAddress}, /tokens/v1/{chainId}/{tokenAddresses} (up to 30 comma-separated), plus marketing/order surfaces (/token-profiles, /token-boosts, /ads, /orders) and sector "metas" endpoints (/metas/trending/v1, /metas/meta/v1/{slug}). Pair payload carries baseToken/quoteToken, priceNative/priceUsd, txns (buys/sells per window), volume, priceChange, liquidity.usd, fdv, marketCap, pairCreatedAt, labels, boosts. Rate limits are tiered: 60 req/min for profile/meta, 300 req/min for pair/token.
- **Key pattern:** Free keyless multi-chain pair/OHLCV-equivalent feed covering Ethereum, BSC, Base, Solana (not RH-4663 via API). Batch token-address lookups (30-addrs) cut request count; separation of 60/min vs 300/min tiers shows which endpoints to warm a cache with; "metas" aggregates tokens into sectors with m5/h1/h6/h24 marketCap change as a momentum read.
- **For Memeland:** Natural keyless fallback/cross-check to GMGN for the EVM+Solana chains GMGN already covers, reducing single-vendor coupling without adding a key; useful as a second opinion for the liquidity/volume/liquidity-drain voters. Data largely overlaps GMGN, and no RH-4663 coverage; batch and rate-tier patterns worth mirroring in Memeland's market-data layer.
- **Costs:** Free, keyless, rate-limited (60-300/min); no paid dependency. Low complexity; means another poller/cache unless Memeland already wraps DexScreener.
- **Verdict:** Study <keyless free multi-chain fallback + rate-tier/batch patterns; data overlaps GMGN and lacks RH-4663>
- **Security/complexity flags:** keyless public endpoint, tiered rate limits, GitBook docs; verdict-ready study-as-secondary/fallback not new edge.
### SRC-030 market-data - Etherscan DEX Tracker
- **What it is:** Etherscan's DEX Tracker (etherscan.io/dextracker, also /dex) is an Ethereum-mainnet-only dashboard that tracks all transactions and liquidity trading pairs from a supported DEX list. Two tabs: Transactions (token transferred in/out, USD value, DEX) and Trading Pairs (token, pair, token price USD, liquidity USD, 24h volume, pair-created time, token contract), with CSV export. Page is behind Etherscan's bot protection (curl HTTP 000); content from the official Etherscan info-center knowledge base.
- **Key pattern:** Simple pair-level tabular snapshot (price, liquidity, 24h volume, creation time) with CSV export; pairs Beta. No documented programmatic API for the tracker itself - the broader Etherscan API is account/token/contract oriented (stats, tokensupply, txlist).
- **For Memeland:** Purely Ethereum and UI-driven; no feed endpoint to consume. Data (pair creation time, liquidity, 24h vol) is fully redundant with GMGN/DexScreener on the Ethereum leg and gives no memecoin/RH-4663 edge. Pattern of surfacing pair-created + liquidity + 24h vol is already in Memeland's voter inputs.
- **Costs:** Free; requires scraping the dashboard (bot-blocked) since there is no tracker API; high friction, low value.
- **Verdict:** Skip <Ethereum-only UI dashboard, no programmatic feed, redundant with GMGN/DexScreener>
- **Security/complexity flags:** bot-protected HTML page, no API for tracker; verdict-ready skip as redundant data surface.
### SRC-034 market-data - GeckoTerminal API (CoinGecko on-chain)
- **What it is:** GeckoTerminal is the CoinGecko DEX/DeFi aggregator exposing a keyless public REST API v2 (api.geckoterminal.com) tracking price, volume, liquidity, transactions, and OHLCV across 200+ networks, 1,500+ DEXes, 6M+ tokens. Docs at apiguide.geckoterminal.com (Mintlify, /llms.txt + .md). Free tier is 30 calls/min; higher limits come via any CoinGecko paid plan (same /onchain endpoints). Endpoints: /networks, /networks/{network}/dexes, /networks/{network}/pools, /networks/{network}/new_pools, /networks/new_pools (cross-network), /networks/{network}/tokens/{token}/pools, batch token-address pool lookups (up to 30), top pools, and per-pool OHLCV candles. Pool fields include reserve_in_usd, base/quote native-currency price, pool_created_at, and include=base_token,quote_token,dex for related data.
- **Key pattern:** Keyless multi-chain DEX feed with new-pools discovery (per-network and cross-network), pool-created-at timestamps, and per-pool OHLCV - the shape GMGN-like screens need. 30-cpm free cap favors batch/address lookups and caching; versioned requests (Beta, subject to change).
- **For Memeland:** Strong complementary keyless source for the EVM+Solana legs that overlaps but can cross-check GMGN and cover new-pool discovery. However api.geckoterminal.com and geckoterminal.com are unreachable from this network (HTTP 000 geo/bot block), and RH-4663 coverage is unconfirmed. Value is Study: use as a second keyless DEX feed / new-pool watcher where reachable, mirroring the batch + include patterns in Memeland's market layer.
- **Costs:** Free 30 cpm keyless; paid only for higher limits via CoinGecko. Low complexity; adds a reachability caveat (blocked in some regions).
- **Verdict:** Study <keyless multi-chain DEX/new-pool feed complementing GMGN; geo-blocked here and no RH-4663 confirmed>
- **Security/complexity flags:** keyless REST, versioned Beta, 30-cpm cap, geo-block observed; verdict-ready study-as-secondary/fallback feed.
### SRC-239 chain-data - Santiment GraphQL DEX Trades metric surface
- **What it is:** graphs.santiment.net/dex_trades is a Dash/Plotly SPA shell (react-entry-point + _dash-config) serving the Santiment "DEX Trades" GraphQL playground - the concrete GraphQL surface for on-chain decentralized-exchange trade data behind Santiment's SanAPI (same vendor/account as SRC-011). The page itself is an interactive UI, not a keyless endpoint; programmatic access requires a Santiment API key via the SanAPI GraphQL gateway.
- **Key pattern:** GraphQL metric schema for DEX trades (asset/interval/aggregation across trades) - shows how Santiment models on-chain trade streams into queryable time-series metrics, a shape worth knowing even if the data source is gated.
- **For Memeland:** Same vendor and caveats as SRC-011: paid SanAPI key, ~2000-3000 tracked assets, no confirmed RH-4663 coverage, weak long-tail/memecoin fit. The DEX-trades metric overlaps what GMGN already provides on Memeland's chains; no new keyless edge.
- **Costs:** Requires paid Santiment SanAPI key; SPA only (no keyless JSON); redundant with existing GMGN trade data on the covered chains.
- **Verdict:** Study <reconfirms Santiment SanAPI DEX-trades GraphQL shape; paywalled and no RH, redundant with GMGN on covered chains>
- **Security/complexity flags:** key-gated GraphQL, Dash SPA shell, no keyless feed; verdict-ready study-as-pattern only.
### SRC-243 chain-data - liquidity.vision (Uniswap v3 LP tool, unreachable)
- **What it is:** liquidity.vision was a standalone web dashboard for visualizing and managing Uniswap v3 liquidity positions (interactive price-range charts over LP positions, position value, uncollected fees). It is now effectively discontinued: the domain is unreachable from this network (curl HTTP 000 / connection timeout), and no API, key, or service endpoint is documented or discoverable via web search.
- **Key pattern:** Historical LP-position management UI pattern (position token amounts, fee tiers, in/out-of-range price bands, impermanent-loss readout); these capabilities now live natively in Uniswap's own app and third-party position trackers.
- **For Memeland:** No transferable data feed or API; it is an Ethereum/Uniswap-v3 LP management tool unrelated to Memeland's memecoin sniper/trade side, and it is dead. Adding or emulating LP-position management is out of scope for a sniper bot with no LP position to manage.
- **Costs:** None; site unreachable, no integration possible.
- **Verdict:** Skip <dead/unreachable Uniswap-v3 LP management UI, no API, no relevance to memecoin trading edge>

### SRC-258 market-data - CoinGecko "Best Free Crypto APIs" comparison (editorial)
- **What it is:** A CoinGecko Learn editorial comparing free crypto data APIs in 2026 (CoinGecko, CoinMarketCap, CoinPaprika, CoinAPI, DexScreener, plus Binance/Coinbase exchange APIs). It frames selection criteria: asset/market coverage, keyless vs keyed access, free credits vs credit-consume model, endpoint availability on free tier, rate limits/refresh, historical depth, and onchain/DEX coverage. Key table data: CoinGecko keyless 10k calls/mo @100cpm; CoinMarketCap keyless 15k credits/mo @50cpm; CoinPaprika keyed 20k/mo @10cps; CoinAPI keyed  one-time @~1cps/100-day; DexScreener keyless @60cpm; Binance/Coinbase public market data keyless with WS.
- **Key pattern:** A decision framework (coverage x keyless x credit-model x rate x history x DEX/onchain) for choosing/stacking market-data providers - directly useful when deciding which keyless feeds to add as GMGN redundancy and which to skip as keyed/redundant. Article itself is content, not a data endpoint.
- **For Memeland:** Validates the keyless options (CoinGecko, DexScreener, GeckoTerminal, Binance/Coinbase WS) as cheap multi-chain fallbacks and re-confirms CoinPaprika/CoinAPI as keyed. It provides the selection lens (flat-credit vs per-volume metering, cached-response not charging) rather than a concrete feed; no RH-4663 info and no memecoin-specific depth.
- **Costs:** Free to read; no integration. Actionable only as a reference for the feed-selection tradeoffs Memeland should already weigh for its market-data layer.
- **Verdict:** Study <useful comparative selection framework for keyless feed redundancy, not a data feed; no RH-4663 detail>
- **Security/complexity flags:** vendor editorial (biased to CoinGecko), no API surface; verdict-ready study-as-reference only.
### SRC-259 market-data - GeckoTerminal web product (token discovery + new pools)
- **What it is:** GeckoTerminal (CoinGecko's DEX aggregator, geckoterminal.com) is the web product behind the API in SRC-034: real-time price, volume, transactions, and liquidity across 1,500+ DEXes and 200+ networks, 6M+ tokens. UI surfaces network and DEX explorers, multichart technical-analysis tools, watchlists, and a "New Pools" section listing recently created liquidity pools (with swap links) - the classic early-token-discovery workflow. Regionally geo-blocked here (curl HTTP 000), so researched via docs and third-party reviews.

Coverage breadth (200+ networks) may or may not include RH-4663 (not confirmed) and the site/API are unreachable from this network. Product value is largely the same data the SRC-034 API exposes.
- **Costs:** Free UI + keyless API; no paid key needed. Site geo-blocked here; API cap 30 cpm free.
- **Verdict:** Study <product pattern for new-pool/early-token discovery and deep multi-chain coverage; geo-blocked here, RH-4663 unconfirmed, overlaps SRC-034>
- **Security/complexity flags:** free keyless product, geo-block observed, no RH confirmation; verdict-ready study-as-discovery-pattern/secondary feed.
### SRC-260 market-data - The Block DEX volume dashboard (paywalled)
- **What it is:** theblockcrypto.com/data/open-finance/dex-non-custodial is The Block's data page for decentralized-exchange activity - a monthly dashboard of aggregate DEX spot-trading volume (flash-trade filtered), a non-custodial DEX-to-CEX volume share series, and derived macro metrics. Landing page is Cloudflare-403-blocked from this network (curl returns HTTP 403 'blocked'); the substantive series sit behind The Block Pro's 'Request Data' / subscription gate, with only headline charts public.

No memecoin or RH-4663 coverage, no API shape to copy, fully gated behind a paid subscription.

n- **Security/complexity flags:** Cloudflare bot-block, subscription-gated data, no keyless endpoint; verdict-ready skip.

### SRC-023 market-data - Codex unified GraphQL crypto data API (RH-4663 covered)
- **What it is:** docs.codex.io is the docs for Codex (codex.io / @codex-data), a unified GraphQL + WebSocket API for token prices, trades, liquidity pools, OHLCV bars, holders, wallet analytics, prediction markets, and launchpad data across 100+ EVM and non-EVM networks. Endpoint 'https://graph.codex.io/graphql' (REST-style HTTP POST + graphql-ws subscriptions), auth via 'Authorization: <API_KEY>' header or pay-per-request over Machine Payments Protocol (HTTP 402). First-party TypeScript SDK '@codex-data/sdk'; docs also ship an llms.txt and an AI-agent skill/MCP server.
- **Key pattern:** One GraphQL schema over many chains with a required 'networkId' filter; rich batched queries (getTokenPrices/tokens accept arrays to batch instead of loop). Most importantly the supported-networks table is generated live from the API and explicitly lists Robinhood = chain id 4663, plus Solana (1399811149), Base, BNB, Ethereum, Arbitrum - i.e. full coverage of every chain Memeland trades. Subscriptions give a push model for real-time trades/holders. MPP (Machine Payments Protocol) lets agents pay over HTTP 402 without a standing key.
- **For Memeland:** This is the rare market-data provider that natively covers RH-4663 (Robinhood L2, chain 4663) AND Solana/Base/BSC/ETH through one consistent GraphQL surface - a genuine lever to reduce GMGN coupling while keeping RH chain coverage. Wallet analytics + holders + launchpad + prediction-market fields plug directly into the smart-money/liquidity/bot-risk voter inputs; OHLCV and trades support the calibration and fill-simulation modules; the WebSocket subscription shape is portable to Memeland's event loop. The @codex-data/sdk is a copyable TypeScript pattern for a typed multi-chain data client.
- **Costs:** Paid keyed API (Bucketed/subscription) or per-request MPP; it is another commercial vendor alongside GMGN, so it only reduces coupling if it can cleanly de-position our GMGN feed on a chain - otherwise it adds a second paid dependency rather than removing one. RH coverage breadth should be spot-verified (chain is new, curved/liquidity depth may be thin).
- **Verdict:** Adopt <unified keyless-to-MPP GraphQL feed with explicit RH-4663 + all Memeland chains covered and wallet/launchpad analytics; strongest candidate to reduce GMGN coupling>
- **Security/complexity flags:** paid key dependency (mitigated by MPP), third-party indexing trust, new-chain depth unverified; verdict-ready adopt-as-primary-feed-candidate.

### SRC-007 chain-data-infra - Alchemy blockchain infrastructure (RH Arc + 100 chains)`n- **What it is:** Alchemy is a hosted blockchain-infrastructure provider across 100+ chains: RPC/JSON-RPC APIs, data APIs, webhooks, enhanced APIs, devtools (faucets, sandbox), and rollup/validator offerings. Landing page and RPC directory confirm support for Robinhood Chain (Arc) - /rpc/robinhood-testnet lists Chain ID 46630 for testnet while the site banner states "Arc mainnet is live on Alchemy," pairing with the Robinhood-chain branding alongside Solana RPC, gRPC, and streaming lanes.`n- **Key pattern:** Standard multi-chain JSON-RPC + enhanced-API pattern; per-chain RPC resource pages with chain id, token, and endpoints; free-tier get-started flow; the Debug/archive and websocket capabilities familiar from the already-adopted RH fast-submit/Nitro/Quoter/txlock execution stack.`n- **For Memeland:** Useful as a resilient fallback RPC provider for RH-4663 mainnet and testnet if the primary node is degraded, and as a model for portable per-chain RPC resource configuration. No memecoin scoring, no keyless data feed - it re-supplies the same RPC/archive primitives already in use.`n- **Costs:** Paid keyed RPC (free tier exists but limits are low); adds an external key + platform dependency with minor portability gain.`n- **Verdict:** Study <provider option; real RH-4663 RPC/archive coverage worth a fallback, but paid key and redundant with existing execution path>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed auth, third-party API key surface; verdict-ready study-as-provider-option.`n
### SRC-014 chain-data-infra - Chainstack managed nodes (generic chain coverage)`n- **What it is:** Chainstack is a managed blockchain-node infrastructure provider: bare-metal/cloud hosted nodes, RPC endpoints, archive access, and multi-provider failover across many networks. The landing page chain list includes Robinhood (Arc) alongside Arbitrum, BNB, Base, Polygon, TRON, Sui and the new EVM chains (Zora, Monad, MegaETH, etc.) through a create-endpoint flow.`n- **Key pattern:** Standard hosted-node + RPC model with network-chainlist enumeration; DevNet/archive tiers and regions - the same primitives as Alchemy/QuickNode rather than a distinct data or signal surface.`n- **For Memeland:** Another generic RPC-node option; adds RH Arc availability but no differentiated data feed, no memecoin/launchpad analytics, and no keyless multi-chain market data. Redundant with the incumbent RPC/QuickNode/provider stack and adds a paid key.`n- **Costs:** Paid node hosting per endpoint; operational rather than informational value; single purpose (RPC) easily duplicated by competitors.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <generic managed-node provider; RH node is the only marginal point and it is redundant with incumbent RPC infra>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed node access, extra external infra; verdict-ready skip as code-to-copy.`n
### SRC-251 chain-data-infra - QuickNode blockchain development platform (RH-4663 mainnet+archive+debug)`n- **What it is:** QuickNode is a multi-chain RPC/data infrastructure platform (nodes, real-time Streams, webhooks, enhanced/indexed data, with a trading-execution angle in newer positioning). Its Robinhood Chain product page explicitly confirms mainnet chain ID 4663 (plus testnet), full archive nodes with no pruning, the Debug API for debug_traceTransaction / debug_traceBlockByNumber, Streams & Webhooks, and an Orbit/L2 profile settling to Ethereum with ETH gas - plus a 500B+ requests SLA and private endpoints.`n- **Key pattern:** The Debug-API + archive pattern is directly relevant: trace-level RPC endpoints expose internal transaction/execution structure for fill simulation, slippage modeling, and MEV-style analysis - the exact primitives Memeland already leans on for RH fast-submit/Nitro/Quoter/txlock. Streams/Webhooks offer a push pattern for monitoring.`n- **For Memeland:** Most complete RH-4663 RPC story among the surveyed providers (mainnet archive + debug_traceTransaction). Useful as a backup execution-observability provider and a reference design for how to structure chain-specific RPC config for RH. No token analytics/launchpad - it is infra, not a data feed.`n- **Costs:** Paid keyed private endpoints (pay-as-you-go tiers); adds an external key dependency that competes with the already-adopted RH execution stack rather than extending it.`n- **Verdict:** Study <provider option with the most explicit RH-4663 mainnet + debug/archive coverage; good backup + reference, not raw data edge>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed auth, cost-metered usage; verdict-ready study-as-provider-option.`n
### SRC-240 chain-data-infra - Helius Solana RPCs, gRPC, webhooks (Solana-only)`n- **What it is:** Helius is a Solana-focused RPC/API platform: RPC endpoints, enhanced APIs, gRPC streaming, webhooks, DAS/NFT and compressed-NFT APIs, and dedicated infrastructure. Landing/meta confirm it is positioned as the leading Solana RPC and API platform with a 1-click free start.`n- **Key pattern:** Solana gRPC streaming plus webhook push model for real-time on-chain events; richer Solana-specific data (token metadata, account indexing) than generic RPC.`n- **For Memeland:** Good Solana-side data tooling, but Memeland already covers Solana via GMGN and the established sniper/execution stack; paid keyed and Solana-only - it does not cover RH-4663 or other chains and adds an extra Solana vendor instead of reducing coupling.`n- **Costs:** Paid API key (rate-limited free tier); single-chain scope; another external dependency.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <paid Solana-only infra redundant with incumbent Solana/GMGN path and no RH or cross-chain value>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed access, third-party Solana trust; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-252 chain-data-infra - Shyft Solana gRPC and staked RPCs (Solana-only)`
n- **What it is:** Shyft provides Solana gRPC nodes, RabbitStream shred streaming, and staked RPCs across 7 global regions with multi-node failover, slot replay, and unlimited streaming with no credits or bandwidth limits, from $199 per month. It targets Solana traders needing low-latency streamed data.`n- **Key pattern:** Slot-level shred streaming plus staked (validator-incentivized) RPCs yield highly reliable low-latency Solana event data - a strong push-subscription transport for on-chain activity.`n- **For Memeland:** Solana-only, paid, and premium-priced infra. The fast-stream concept maps to Solana trading, but Memeland already has a Solana execution/streaming path and Shyft adds neither RH-4663 nor multi-chain market data nor keyless coverage.`n- **Costs:** $199+ per month, no free tier; infrastructure-only value.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <premium paid Solana-only streaming infra, redundant and no RH or multi-chain edge>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** high monthly cost, keyed infra trust; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-255 chain-data-infra - The Graph indexing protocol (GraphQL subgraphs)`
n- **What it is:** The Graph is a decentralized indexing protocol that indexes blockchain events into GraphQL subgraphs so developers can query on-chain data without running their own indexer. It offers an ecosystem-wide graph across many EVM chains via hosted and decentralized networks.`n- **Key pattern:** Subgraph DSL (entities plus mappings) turns raw event logs into queryable GraphQL - a clean schema-driven indexing pattern; the subgraph-plus-GraphQL shape is the archetype behind many data APIs (for example the Codex/docs.codex.io API in SRC-023).`n- **For Memeland:** The subgraph/GraphQL indexing pattern is the intellectual ancestor of several feeds Memeland could use, but running and maintaining custom subgraphs is heavy, adds infrastructure, and there is no confirmed RH-4663 indexer. It is redundant with GMGN plus lightweight REST/GraphQL feeds for the memecoin use case.`n- **Costs:** GraphQL queries are effectively network-hosted, but publishing and maintaining a subgraph is real operational overhead.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <heavy indexing protocol, no RH-4663, redundant pattern better served by direct APIs>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** indexing maintenance burden, trust in decentralized indexers; verdict-ready skip as code-to-copy.`n
### SRC-256 chain-data-infra - Triton Solana RPC and validator infra (Solana-only)`
n- **What it is:** Triton is a Solana-specialist infrastructure provider: production-grade RPCs, streaming, ledger queries, and validator services with 99.99% uptime, open-source ethos, around since 2021.`n- **Key pattern:** Reliable Solana RPC, ledger-query, and validator stack with streamed consensus data - a typical keyed chain-infra offering.`n- **For Memeland:** Solana-only and paid keyed; overlaps GMGN Solana coverage and the existing execution path. It provides no RH-4663 or cross-chain data and no keyless feed.`n- **Costs:** Paid RPC plans; operational rather than data value.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <paid Solana-only provider, redundant with incumbent infra and no RH or multi-chain value>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed access, extra dependency; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-238 chain-data-infra - Goldsky real-time indexing and streaming (stablecoin focus)`
n- **What it is:** Goldsky is a real-time blockchain data platform - subgraph indexing, Mirror streaming pipelines, a GraphQL query/API layer, and RPC infra - positioning around financial products built on stablecoins and high-throughput on-chain data.`n- **Key pattern:** Stream/pipe model that turns raw chain data into queryable APIs; the Mirror pipeline plus subgraph and webhooks push model is a more turnkey variant of The Graph.`n- **For Memeland:** Compelling general streaming architecture, but no confirmed RH-4663 indexer, stablecoin/enterprise focus rather than memecoin discovery, and paid keyed. It is redundant with the lighter REST/GraphQL DEX feeds Memeland already anchors on.`n- **Costs:** Paid keyed platform (usage metered); significant setup to index exactly the pools and contracts Memeland needs.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <enterprise streaming/indexing platform, no RH-4663, heavy and redundant with direct DEX APIs>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** metered and paid, indexing maintenance; verdict-ready skip as code-to-copy.`n
### SRC-012 research - LLM multi-agent system for crypto portfolio mgmt (arXiv 2501.00826v3)`n- **What it is:** A UCL/NTU research paper proposing a multi-agent system for automated crypto portfolio management: three modality-specialised agents (a Crypto Agent processing market/on-chain dynamics, a News Agent encoding weekly news sentiment, a Trading Agent fusing all signals for execution), run over one of three communication architectures (hierarchical, collaborative, debate) and one of four capability configurations (zero-shot, chain-of-thought, RAG, skill-augmented), each with a rolling memory window and a ReAct-style traceable-reasoning prompt.`n- **Key pattern:** In a 52-week 2025 backtest over the top-15 L1 tokens, the best config (Hierarchical + Skill) hit +133.52% cumulative return and 1.502 Sharpe, beating single-agent, passive, and deep-learning baselines; a controlled ablation shows the Crypto (market) Agent is the most critical component (its removal cuts return by 42.57pp); the multi-agent advantage is model-agnostic across GPT-4o, GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4.5. Regime-conditioned evaluation (bull vs bear) is used throughout.`n- **For Memeland:** Validates and sharpens Memeland's existing voter swarm: the ablation insight (the market-data/Crypto agent dominates) argues for weighting/prioritising the data-grounded voters; the regime-conditioned backtest methodology maps directly onto Memeland's calibration/anti-overfit practice; the skill-augmented capability and ReAct traceable-rationale pattern are foldable into voter prompts to keep decisions auditable; the hierarchical-vs-debate architecture question maps to Memeland's layered risk gates and >=80% consensus gate.`n- **Costs:** Pure methodology, no code/dependency to run; requires porting the evaluation design (regime conditioning, ablation, capability configs) into Memeland's existing test harness.`n- **Verdict:** Adapt <transferable methodology: regime-conditioned backtest, ablation, skill-augmentation and agent-weighting patterns to fold into the voter swarm and risk gates>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** research only, no network/key surface; verdict-ready adapt-as-methodology.`n
### SRC-006 market-data - 985monitor (opaque client-rendered app)`
n- **What it is:** 985monitor.xyz is an opaque, client-rendered SPA (localStorage keys such as xMonitorUiLanguageV1/xMonitorThemeModeV1 and an x.com/@Unipioneer link are the only server-side artifacts). No server-rendered content, no visible API/docs, no pricing or product description on the fetched page.`n- **Key pattern:** None extractable - the landing page exposes no data feed, API shape, or architecture to evaluate.`n- **For Memeland:** Nothing transferable; unknown provenance and zero documentation make it a trust/security dead end rather than a candidate source.`n- **Costs:** Unknown/opaque; no clear cost model visible.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <opaque client-only app with no API, docs, or data surface to assess>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** no keyless endpoint, unknown trust model; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-027 trading-platform - Fere AI autonomous trading agents (hosted SaaS)`n- **What it is:** Fere AI (docs.fereai.xyz) is a hosted autonomous crypto-trading-agent platform: agents that research, reason, and execute across chains with a built-in wallet (Coinbase CDP security), scheduled strategy automation with cross-run memory, a fere_tools sandbox SDK catalog, gasless cross-chain bridging/swaps, MCP support to drive Fere from Claude/ChatGPT/Cursor, and credits/subscription billing (Lite/Pro/Whale).`n- **Key pattern:** Product pattern of decomposing requests into agent actions with a wallet, strategy-automation-with-memory, and an MCP interface; all behind a closed, credit-metered platform.`n- **For Memeland:** The strategy-automation-with-memory and MCP-drive concepts are mildly interesting as UX patterns, but Fere is a competing closed trading-agent SaaS (credits/paid) that Memeland would never delegate control to; it contributes no data feed, no reusable library, and no RH-4663 or GMGN-reducing edge.`n- **Costs:** Credit-based paid subscriptions for every action; full external custody/control dependency.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <hosted paid trading-agent platform, closed and redundant with Memeland's own engine, no data or reusable pattern>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** closed custody, paid metered usage, external control plane; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-029 chain-data-infra - Moralis Web3 data API and Streams (keyed EVM)`
n- **What it is:** Moralis (docs.moralis.com, network-blocked from this box - curl HTTP 000) is a hosted Web3 data-API platform: EVM API, Wallet API, Token API, Price API, NFT API, Auth API, plus Streams (real-time webhook pipelines that push on-chain events to your endpoint). Coverage is the mainstream EVM set (Ethereum, BSC, Polygon, Base, Arbitrum, Avalanche, Optimism, etc.) and some non-EVM.`n- **Key pattern:** The Streams model - a server-side watcher that pushes decoded on-chain events over webhooks - is the architectural pattern Memeland already mirrors with its decoupled MarketSentinel risk service and RH txlock/Nitro event paths. The EVM/REST API surface is otherwise conventional keyed data.`n- **For Memeland:** No confirmed RH-4663 coverage (it indexes mainstream EVM, not the new Robinhood L2), it is keyed/paid, and its data overlaps GMGN on Memeland chains. The Streams/webhook push pattern is the only transferable idea and it is already implemented internally.`n- **Costs:** Paid API key with rate-limited free tier; a second external vendor for data GMGN already supplies.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <keyed EVM data API, no confirmed RH-4663, Streams pattern already mirrored by MarketSentinel>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed auth, third-party data trust; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-018 market-data - Shrimpy portfolio/rebalancing API (band-based)`
n- **What it is:** Shrimpy (developers.shrimpy.io, Cloudflare 403 from this box) is a crypto portfolio-management and trading API for exchanges plus self-custody: automated rebalancing (periodic and threshold/band-based), order-balance trading endpoints, portfolio tracking, and Shrimpy Universe market data for 1000+ assets.`n- **Key pattern:** The band-rebalancing and order-balance mechanics are the concrete transferable piece - a rule that triggers trades only when an allocation drifts beyond a tolerance band, keeping turnover and cost bounded while enforcing target weights.`n- **For Memeland:** Band-based rebalancing is portfolio-level sizing logic that could inform Memeland layered risk gates and position sizing across its basket of chains, and the order-balance API shape is a clean execution contract. But it is a keyed/paid SaaS aimed at slower portfolio rebalancing, not short-horizon memecoin sniping; no RH-4663 coverage.`n- **Costs:** Paid API key/subscription; single-purpose rebalancing vendor for a HF bot.`n- **Verdict:** Study <band-rebalancing and order-balance pattern worth adapting into sizing/risk, but keyed SaaS with no RH edge>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed access, external account/order handling; verdict-ready study-as-pattern.`n
### SRC-031 research - AgentStrategy ETHGlobal multi-agent trading showcase`
n- **What it is:** AgentStrategy (ethglobal.com/showcase/agentstrategy-hiyug, fetched OK) is an ETHGlobal Agentic-Ethereum hackathon project: a swarm of autonomous AI agents that analyze, trade, and promote a portfolio of AI-agent tokens on Base. One analyst agent every minute picks one of the top-100 tokens and writes a report covering social metrics (Twitter mindshare, engagement quality, sentiment), technicals (price action, volume, market structure), plus a buy/hold/sell recommendation with a conviction level and suggested time horizon. A separate trader agent every five minutes reads the stored analyst reports plus current holdings/market conditions and decides whether to execute. A third influencer agent drafts promotional tweets. Stack: Cookie.fun API + X posts + OpenAI LLMs + Supabase.`n- **Key pattern:** The analyst/trader separation with an explicit conviction level and suggested time horizon per recommendation is the direct analogue of Memeland voter swarm consensus; the report-as-structured-artifact (persisted in Supabase) pattern keeps analysis reusable and auditable.`n- **For Memeland:** The conviction-level plus time-horizon framing is a clean way to make the existing >=80% voter gate richer (weight votes by conviction, reject stale horizons), and the persisted-analysis-report pattern fits a decoupled analysis store. However it is an MVP on Base only, uses Cookie.fun (AI-agent-token data, not memecoins), OpenAI and Supabase - no production execution, no RH-4663.`n- **Costs:** None to copy (open-source hackathon project); only the report schema and agent-separation ideas transfer.`n- **Verdict:** Study <conviction + time-horizon report schema and analyst/trader separation to enrich the voter gate; MVP on Base, no RH>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** hackathon-grade code, third-party API keys (Cookie/OpenAI/Supabase); verdict-ready study-as-pattern.`n
### SRC-241 chain-data - Bitquery GraphQL blockchain API + IDE explorer`
n- **What it is:** Bitquery (ide.bitquery.io, fetched as a React SPA shell) is a multi-chain blockchain-data GraphQL API covering 40+ networks (Ethereum, BSC, Solana, Polygon, Base, Arbitrum, Tron, and many more) with datasets for DEX trades, DEX trades-by-DEX, tokens, wallets, and on-chain metrics. The IDE is a GraphiQL-style explorer for composing queries; dexrabbit (SRC-021) is its DEX-screener product built on the same data.`n- **Key pattern:** A single GraphQL schema over many chains with rich DEX-trade datasets and wallet/on-chain metrics - the schema shape and DEX-trades query structure are directly portable to Memeland voter inputs.`n- **For Memeland:** The GraphQL DEX-trades/wallet dataset is complementary keyed coverage for Solana/BSC/Base/ETH, but there is no confirmed RH-4663 dataset and it requires a paid key, so it cannot replace GMGN on RH. Worth studying as a schema/API-shape reference and possible multi-chain backup where keyless feeds are thin.`n- **Costs:** Paid API key with tiered pricing; no confirmed RH-4663 coverage.`n- **Verdict:** Study <multi-chain GraphQL DEX-trades API shape to mirror; keyed, no RH-4663, so not an adopt>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed auth, query-cost metering; verdict-ready study-as-pattern.`n
### SRC-021 market-data - DEXRabbit DEX screener by Bitquery (network-blocked)`
n- **What it is:** DEXRabbit (dexrabbit.bitquery.io, curl HTTP 000 from this box) is Bitquery DEX-screener product - a dashboard/explorer for DEX trades, token pairs, and market activity built on top of Bitquery GraphQL data (same backend as SRC-241). It offers multi-chain DEX analytics with token/datasource and sort/filter UI.`n- **Key pattern:** DEX screener UI patterns (sortable pairs, liquidity/volume columns, token discovery) mirror what GMGN and DexScreener already provide; no standalone keyless API is exposed beyond the underlying Bitquery keyed GraphQL.`n- **For Memeland:** Redundant with the incumbent GMGN discovery/analysis surface; the underlying Bitquery schema is covered in SRC-241. No RH-4663 coverage and paid-keyed backend add no new edge.`n- **Costs:** Paid Bitquery key behind it; otherwise nothing to run.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <DEX screener product redundant with GMGN/DexScreener; value only in the keyed Bitquery schema covered by SRC-241>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyed backend, opaque dashboard; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-242 trading-execution - Jupiter DEX aggregator + swap/RFQ API (Solana)`
n- **What it is:** Jupiter (jup.ag, network-blocked curl HTTP 000 from this box) is the primary Solana DEX aggregator and the jup.ag product surface; its public APIs include price, quote/swap, priority-fee, and RFQ flows (plus JLP perps and limit orders). The quote/swap endpoints are keyless and return the route with inAmount/outAmount, priceImpactPct, fees and priority-fee hints.`n- **Key pattern:** The quote-response contract - explicit price-impact percentage, fee breakdown, route, and priority-fee parameter - is exactly the execution-governance shape Memeland already uses for fill simulation and curve math on RH; Jupiter demonstrates a clean, keyless way to structure a multi-pool quote on Solana.`n- **For Memeland:** A keyless Solana swap/quote path is a concrete lever to reduce GMGN dependency on Solana execution and to enrich the curve-math/fill-simulation module with real multi-pool price-impact data. Solana-only (no RH-4663), but the quote schema and priority-fee handling are directly portable design.`n- **Costs:** Free/public endpoints (rate limits apply); adds Solana-only execution dependency, no RH coverage.`n- **Verdict:** Adapt <keyless Solana quote/swap API with price-impact + priority-fee shape that maps to execution governance and reduces GMGN coupling on Solana>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** keyless but rate-limited, third-party route trust; verdict-ready adapt-as-execution-pattern.`n

n- **What it is:** madeonsol.com is a Solana memecoin launchpad/ecosystem (token creation, launches, trading UI); the /developer docs page returned HTTP 403 from this box so its specific API surface could not be read. Solana-only tooling.`n- **Key pattern:** Unverified - the page is Cloudflare-blocked; presumed launchpad/token-metadata/trading API endpoints typical of Solana launchers.`n- **For Memeland:** Likely overlaps GMGN Solana memecoin discovery; Solana-only, no RH-4663, and possibly keyed - so no confirmed feed or API shape that beats the incumbent stack.`n- **Costs:** Unknown/potentially keyed; Solana-only.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <Solana-only launchpad, page inaccessible (403), no confirmed RH/multi-chain or non-GMGN feed>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** Cloudflare-blocked, unverified API surface; verdict-ready skip on unreadable source.`n

n- **What it is:** maverick-backend.onrender.com/api is a bare backend deployed on Render free tier that returned curl HTTP 000 from this box - Render free backends spin down on inactivity, and the page exposes no documentation, API spec, or product context.`n- **Key pattern:** None extractable; a free-tier Render hosting pattern that is unreliable for production and opaque as a data source.`n- **For Memeland:** No transferable data, architecture, or API shape; an anonymous hobby backend with no SLA or docs is not a credible source and adds risk if depended on.`n- **Costs:** Not applicable; unsupported/unknown.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <opaque free-tier backend with no docs/API surface and no SLA>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** unreachable, no auth/trust model, unreliable host; verdict-ready skip.`n
### SRC-250 market-data - Nomics market-data API (deprecated/dead)`
n- **What it is:** p.nomics.com (Nomics price/history product page) returned curl HTTP 000 from this box. Nomics was a market-data API provider (free tier plus keyed paid plans for prices, market caps, OHLCV, exchanges); the service was deprecated/discontinued after its acquisition and the free API was shut down.`n- **Key pattern:** Historically a conventional REST market-data API (candles, markets, exchange rates) - now discontinued; the only lesson is a deprecated-vendor cautionary tale.`n- **For Memeland:** No live feed or API shape remains to adopt; entirely redundant with current keyless feeds (CoinGecko, GeckoTerminal, DexPaprika) that already cover Memeland chains.`n- **Costs:** Dead product; nothing to run or pay.`n- **Verdict:** Skip <deprecated/dead market-data API with no live surface, fully redundant with incumbent keyless feeds>`n- **Security/complexity flags:** endpoint dead (000), unmaintained vendor; verdict-ready skip.`n
